# Code audit — 2026-09-01

Read-only audit of the tree at `testing @ 56863d2` + the uncommitted 20-file working set (exe 11348715 deployed; suite 2388 passed / 2 skipped).
Method: 12 parallel readers, one lens each (architecture seams, two-path drift, pass contract, tunables, settings plumbing, swallowed failures, I/O + determinism, tests, harnesses, performance, docs, packaging), every reader handed the project's rules and `feedback_method_traps`. 124 raw findings, 121 after dedupe. Then one adversarial verifier per finding (full re-derivation on the 26 highs, triage on the rest) judging three questions: is it real, is it already known/killed/fixed, does the proposed fix respect the rules. **0 refuted; 76 confirmed as stated, 45 confirmed in substance with a corrected number, scope or severity.** Prior art: 67 new, 51 already recorded as open, 3 recorded as accepted design decisions. Re-graded: 18 high, 83 medium, 20 low.

No code was changed. No conversion was run — every geometry/behaviour item carries the A/B recipe that must run before it is acted on.

## Executive summary — the 18 highs

- **F001** convert_one_armor.py exits 0 on a piece whose shape was dropped or whose fit pass raised; the batch CLI does not, and two of four A/B wrappers gate on that exit code alone — `scripts/convert_one_armor.py:238` (S/none)
- **F002** Six tracked gates exit 0 on an empty measured set; golden_output's TOOL_MAP floor flag is a docstring false positive — `scripts/analysis/verify_zero_weight_bones.py:117` (S/none)
- **F003** Both weight workers read-modify-write the ONE shared per-piece physics XML; six weight passes plus the reauthor preserve-gate read it from the DESTINATION mid-race (source-bound text is fallback only) -- the named-but-untested candidate for the 53 residual weight rows — `src/nif_convert.py:24665` (M/behaviour)
- **F005** PRECONDITION A: six structure guards are coupled to the single filename nif_convert.py; a moved pass is dropped by five of them without any test failing, and the sibling blindness is already live for fit_metrics.minimum_push — `scripts/pass_map.py:174` (M/none)
- **F006** PRECONDITION B: the `nc.*` namespace IS the test and sibling API (~156 monkeypatch sites over 56 names, 78 reload sites in 22 files, 254 private-name refs, no __all__) -- a star-import shim re-exports none of it, and patches only reach callers whose code still lives in nif_convert.py — `src/nif_convert.py:73` (L/vert-neutral)
- **F008** install_mo2_entry.ps1 -InstallTools Remove-Items the live tools dir (settings + 13 backups + logs); the /MIR class deploy_exe.ps1 was written to end, still advertised by build_exe.ps1 and by memory project_cbbe_to_ube.md:135 — `scripts/install_mo2_entry.ps1:70` (S/none)
- **F009** The exe carries no build identity: '1.3' has shipped on 12 distinct builds (8 committed + 4 uncommitted), the deployed exe is 4 builds past the last committed blob, and the three hand-kept CRC records disagree (946DB86E / E14551C6 / 11348715) — `src/version.py:27` (M/none)
- **F010** Copy-path early panel-rigidity is body-blind with no downstream repair; the global OFF verdict was measured on body-swap only -- re-measure the per-path split on the CURRENT tree before defaulting it — `src/nif_convert.py:7109` (M/geometry)
- **F011** conform (and the groove authored-cap) read a ZERO source standoff at defaults while the same function already recomputes the normals for the chest pass; the fix is built and reachable but a recorded collider regression (461->467) and the owed conform retune block the flip, not a missing census — `src/nif_convert.py:1706` (L/geometry)
- **F012** NIF work is dispatched per mod behind a blocking barrier; the cross-mod queue planned in OPTIMIZATION_LOG (a ~20% CEILING on a stale 07-30 baseline) is unimplemented — `src/auto_convert.py:1797` (L/behaviour)
- **F014** The shipped chord fix and its surface-deficit sibling return None on ANY exception -- the caller reads that as 'no chord demand', and the structural guard test cannot see either handler — `src/nif_convert.py:4469` (S/vert-neutral)
- **F015** Voiced caller, mute callee: the three smoothing helpers swallow their whole body and return the input, so the caller-side `_note_pass_failure` wrappers can never fire -- and 7 of their 10 call sites have no wrapper at all — `src/nif_convert.py:4825` (S/vert-neutral)
- **F016** `_copy_shape`'s shader transplant (effect-shader glow AND lighting-shader field copy) fails silently: ships white/static glow or default shader values, and un-gates the glow CTD skip — `src/nif_convert.py:23311` (S/vert-neutral)
- **F019** Inside `_finalize_hdt_physics` the authored-XML copy is `except Exception: pass` and the resulting `return False` is discarded: the piece ships with an UNHARDENED XML and no collider re-import, and the source-of-truth binding hides it from every diagnostic — `src/nif_convert.py:24714` (S/vert-neutral)
- **F020** Neither convert path is reached by any collected test: convert_nif / convert_nif_phase2 / _nif_convert_worker have zero pytest callers (probe-verified), and the one caller lives in a __main__-gated, zero-collecting script — `tests/test_m3_belt.py:76` (L/none)
- **F021** 27 PASS_MAP passes (plus 5 named only as parity-allowlist strings) and 77 of 308 nif_convert functions are never named by any test; the default-ON seed-spine-anchors, chest layered-cloth sync, coherence repair, short-edge cap and the 274-line final validator have no behavioural guard, and the shipped bust-chord helper executes in-suite only as an un-asserted side effect — `src/nif_convert.py:4383` (L/none)
- **F024** Copy path's 258-line fit try discards warp/groove/snap/chain-blend into `snapped = None` with no record; phase 2 records each; the ratchet test exempts assignment-only handlers by definition — `src/nif_convert.py:7183` (S/vert-neutral)
- **F025** Copy-path `_copy_shape` retry silently ships source verts + source skin as 'converted (copy)'; the fallback may also duplicate a half-built shape; phase 2 drops instead — `src/nif_convert.py:7417` (S/vert-neutral)

## What the highs say, grouped

- **Failures vanish (F014 F015 F016 F019 F024 F025):** the shipped chord fix returns `None` on any exception and the caller reads that as 'no demand'; the smoothing helpers swallow their body; `_copy_shape`'s shader/glow transplant is `except: pass`; the copy path discards its whole fit chain into `snapped = None` and ships source verts as `converted (copy)` where phase 2 records or DROPs. The guard test that exists sees 0 of 227 silent handlers (F081). These are all S-effort, vert-neutral: add the voice, then a raise-injection test (F085).
- **Harness exit codes lie (F001 F002):** the single-piece harness exits 0 on a dropped shape or a raised pass, and two A/B tools gate on that code; six gate tools PASS on zero items. Every verdict measured through them inherits the hole.
- **The monolith split has three preconditions (F005 F006 F007)** before any function moves: widen the six single-file guards, treat the `nc.*` namespace as an API (156 monkeypatch sites), and keep the four `global`-rebound singletons on `nc`. Then the 9-module leaf-first order (F048–F058).
- **Deploy identity (F008 F009):** `-InstallTools` deletes the live settings file; the exe cannot say which build it is (12 builds shipped as '1.3'), so every in-game verdict is attributed by hand.
- **Producer defects with measured levers (F010 F011 F012):** copy-path early panel rigidity is body-blind with nothing downstream; conform reads a zero source standoff while the normal fix is confirmed in game but pinned off; the cross-mod queue is the one measured 20% speed item, unbuilt. All need a convert A/B first.
- **Nobody tests the entry points (F020 F021):** zero collected tests call `convert_nif`/`convert_nif_phase2`; 27 passes and 77 functions are named by no test.
- **The XML race (F003):** both weight workers read-modify-write one shared physics XML — the best candidate for the 53 nondeterministic weight rows; mechanism test recipe in the block.

## Recommended order

1. Voice the silent handlers + harness exit codes + zero-item floors (F001 F002 F014–F019 F024 F025 F081 F083 F085). All S, none move a vertex. Ratchet test after.
2. Build stamp + settings-safe installer + effective-config dump (F008 F009 F076 F004 F064). Ends hand-attribution of verdicts.
3. Docs/comments that would mislead an edit (F027–F035 F089 F031). Cheap, and several are traps recorded in memory already.
4. Split preconditions (F005 F006 F007 F059 F087), then modules leaf-first (F049 F050 first — pure leaves).
5. Geometry items via one exe-parity harness each, both weights, then in game (F010 F011 F094 F068 F101 F098).
6. Two-path unification (F102 F103) only after 4 — it is the same move.

## 1. Safe now — tools/tests/docs or provably vert-neutral, S/M effort  (83 items)

### F001 [HIGH] convert_one_armor.py exits 0 on a piece whose shape was dropped or whose fit pass raised; the batch CLI does not, and two of four A/B wrappers gate on that exit code alone
`scripts/convert_one_armor.py:238` · effort S · risk none · lens harness-hygiene · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=new, fix=reframe, confidence=high

**Claim.** The producer (convert_one_armor.py, the ONLY sanctioned single-piece path per feedback_single_batch_parity) reports a partially-built mesh as success; the knowledge that this is a failure lives in two consumers' log-greps and is absent from the other two. Against rule 1 (fix the producer, not each consumer) and it is the exact class the memory says produced three wrong verdicts: in coherence_repair_ab / morphtri_gate_ab an arm with a missing shape is diffed against its partner and counted as 'moved' or 'clean'.

**Evidence.** convert_one_armor.py:238-240: `r = ac._nif_convert_worker(item)` / `_say(f"  {stem}{w}: {getattr(r, 'status', r)}" + (f"  -- {r.reason}" ...))` — the loop ends, main() returns None, exit 0 regardless of status/reason. The reason it would need to act on is set at src/nif_convert.py:30141 `result_reason = f"errors during shape copy: {failed}"` on a result whose status is still a converted status (auto_convert.py:770/774 `status == "converted (copy)"|"converted (body-swap)"`). Consumers: inflate_census.py:335-356 docstring "A ZERO EXIT IS NOT SUCCESS: the converter reports `errors during shape copy` ... and still exits 0 ... has cost this project three wrong verdicts" and greps `blob.count("errors during shape copy")`; survival_sweep.py:164 greps the same string. coherence_repair_ab.py:117-124 and morphtri_gate_ab.py:81-88 (token-identical, Jaccard 1.00) return only `r.returncode`, and their callers gate on it alone: coherence_repair_ab.py:180 `if off[0] or on[0]:` / morphtri_gate_ab.py:138 `if on[0] or off[0]:`. grep 'shape copy' over scripts/: 4 hits, all in inflate_census/survival_sweep.

**Verifier.** CONFIRMED core: scripts/convert_one_armor.py:225-240 loops `_0`/`_1`, calls `ac._nif_convert_worker(item)`, prints `status`+`reason` via `_say` and main() falls off -> exit 0 for every result. The reason carries `errors during shape copy: {failed}` (src/nif_convert.py:30141) and, since 07-31, every swallowed pass exception as `PASS FAILED ...` (nif_convert.py:30319-30322 `_pf = _piece_pass_failures()...`), while status stays `converted (copy)` (:7797) / `converted (body-swap)` (:30327). The only non-zero exits in the harness are the three `sys.exit(2)` config errors (:166/:196/:213). The two A/B wrappers (scripts/analysis/coherence_repair_ab.py:117-124, scripts/analysis/morphtri_gate_ab.py:81-88) capture stdout+stderr but gate solely on returncode (:180 `if off[0] or on[0]`, :138 `if on[0] or off[0]`) and use the text only for the slots-0 label / last-line tail, so the failure signal is present and unread. RE-COUNTED (rg --stats, whole repo): 'errors during shape copy' = 6 hits in 4 files; under scripts/ = 4, all in inflate_census.py (:336,:351,:451) and survival_sweep.py (:164) - matches the finding. CORRECTIONS: (1) wrapper paths are scripts/analysis/, not scripts/. (2) The 'missing shape counted as moved/clean' sub-claim is wrong in mechanism: a DROPPED shape (nif_convert.py:7425 `DROPPED (copy failed)`) is absent from the output NIF and falls OUT of `set(soff) & set(son) & set(ssrc)` (coherence_repair_ab) / `set(a) & set(b)` (morphtri diff) - silently omitted, not scored. What IS scored as geometry is a shape whose fit pass raised (e.g. :6696 warp-hf, :6719 inflate-hf, :6987 inflate, :7259 survival) and was still written un-fitted. Same conclusion, different vertex population. (3) Even the two 'good' consumers are incomplete: survival_sweep.py:164 and inflate_census.py:351 grep only the shape-copy string, not `PASS FAILED`, which feedback_grep_shape_copy_errors calls 'the better one'. (4) Unstated but strengthening: the BATCH CLI already turns dropped shapes into a non-zero exit (src/auto_convert.py:3210-3217 `overall_failures += r.nif_partial`) and counts PASS FAILED via count_pass_failures (:556). The single-piece harness therefore diverges from the batch's own exit contract - a parity gap, not just hygiene. (5) Commit cf76a1d established that a status print must not abort MID-loop (half-converted pair); an exit code AFTER the loop is compatible with that design, which the fix must preserve. PRIOR ART: no producer-side fix anywhere - git log -S'errors during shape copy' shows only consumer commits (3f01f55 fixed inflate_census's reading; 03382fb survival_sweep); `returncode`/`BROKEN` never appear in convert_one_armor.py; tests/test_single_batch_parity.py asserts only the slots-0 `sys.exit(2)` (L92); phase1_settled.json has 0 hits for 'shape copy'/'convert_one_armor'.

**Fix.** After the `_0`/`_1` loop (not inside it - cf76a1d's no-abort-mid-pair rule) collect the two results and exit non-zero with distinct codes: 3 if any `status == "error"` or `dropped_shapes`, 4 if `reason` contains `PASS FAILED` or `errors during shape copy`; print one machine-readable `BROKEN <n> <kind>` line. Then route coherence_repair_ab / morphtri_gate_ab through one `_census_common.convert_piece(...) -> (rc, out_dir, broken, log)` and extend the two existing greps to also match `PASS FAILED` (they currently miss the string the memory calls the better one).

**Proof it landed.** Harness only, `git diff --stat src/` empty. (1) Extend tests/test_single_batch_parity.py: monkeypatch one fit pass to raise -> exit 4 + `BROKEN 1 pass`; monkeypatch `_copy_shape` to raise twice -> exit 3 + `BROKEN 1 dropped`. (2) Run both A/B wrappers on a piece with an injected failing pass: it must land in `drops["convert_err"]` / print CONVERT FAILED, not a scored row. (3) inflate_census.py:451 counter must read 0 on the population where it read >0. (4) BEHAVIOUR NOTE for survival_sweep.py:158-159: it returns `status: FAILED` on any non-zero rc, so pieces previously reported `ok` with `shape_copy_errors` will move to FAILED - re-derive its status distribution on the last sweep's console.txt files (kept on disk per :168-171) before trusting a post-fix sweep.

**Prior art.** Consumer-side rule only: memory feedback_grep_shape_copy_errors.md; commit 3f01f55 (inflate_census reads the string); survival_sweep.py:164. No producer fix recorded; not in IN_GAME_BUGS.md, phase1_settled.json, or docs/worklog.

### F002 [HIGH] Six tracked gates exit 0 on an empty measured set; golden_output's TOOL_MAP floor flag is a docstring false positive
`scripts/analysis/verify_zero_weight_bones.py:117` · effort S · risk none · lens harness-hygiene · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=known-open, fix=reframe, confidence=high

**Claim.** Six tracked gates cannot distinguish 'nothing is wrong' from 'I measured nothing'. verify_zero_weight_bones.py is the one the memory index orders run after ANY weight pass (project_zeroweight_bone_class.md), and the out_dir-with-a-`meshes`-dir trap (feedback_single_batch_parity) is a realistic way to hand it an empty or wrong tree. The golden case is the documented '6/6 reads as success' trap, still present, and docs/TOOL_MAP.md marks golden_output.py `floor: yes` (see the next finding for why).

**Evidence.** verify_zero_weight_bones.py:66-69 `files = [f for f in glob.glob(str(root / "**" / "*.nif"), recursive=True)]` / `if limit: files = files[:limit]` then :117 `return 1 if total else 0` — no check that files is non-empty; docstring :33 'Exit 1 if any are found, so it can gate a run.' Same shape: verify_weight_invariant.py:69-73 then :112 `return 1 if (tot_over or tot_bad) else 0`; fit_audit.py:312 `nifs = sorted(meshes.rglob("*_1.nif"))` then :394 `sys.exit(1 if n_critical else 0)`; scan_morph_issues.py:260 `nifs = sorted(meshes.rglob("*.nif"))`, main() has no return (exit 0); physics_cloth_health.py:174-175 `if not rows:` / `return 0` — an explicit pass on an empty measured set. golden_output.py:112-113 `except FileNotFoundError:` / `return list(_DEFAULT_PIECES)` silently swaps the piece set; check() :358-361 iterates PIECES and `continue`s on any label missing from the manifest, never counts how many pieces were compared, and :431-434 prints `PASS: output identical to the baseline` whenever `bad == 0` — so with pieces.json gone and a custom baseline, 0 comparisons print PASS. Contrast the tools that DO floor: fold_census.py:176-179 `if not files: ... return 2`, postreconvert_audit.py:246-251, sanity_check_converted.py:213-215.

**Verifier.** All six code paths CONFIRMED at the cited lines. verify_zero_weight_bones.py:66-69 globs with no empty check, :117 `return 1 if total else 0` and prints 'clean -- every bone...' on 0 files; verify_weight_invariant.py:69-73/:112 same shape; fit_audit.py:312 `nifs = sorted(meshes.rglob("*_1.nif"))`, :394 `sys.exit(1 if n_critical else 0)`; scan_morph_issues.py:260 rglob, main() exits only at :258 (missing dir) - the `return` at :364 is inside the nested `section()` helper - so an empty tree exits 0; physics_cloth_health.py:174-175 `if not rows: return 0` (it does print 'MEASURED simulated pieces  : 0' first, so the number is visible but the exit is a pass); golden_output.py:111-113 `except FileNotFoundError: return list(_DEFAULT_PIECES)` silent, check() :358-361 `continue`s on labels absent from manifest with no compared counter, :431-434 `PASS: output identical` whenever `bad == 0`. Golden's TOOL_MAP `floor: yes` (docs/TOOL_MAP.md:52) is a FALSE POSITIVE: tool_map.py:234-245 `_has_floor` substring-matches 'empty set', and the ONLY probe hit in golden_output.py is the docstring at :51 ('returns an empty set'), unrelated to any floor; tests/test_tool_map.py:186-194 pins 3 positive cases and no negative. Contrast floors CONFIRMED at fold_census.py:176-179 (`return 2`), postreconvert_audit.py:246-251 (`NOTHING MEASURED ... return 2`), sanity_check_converted.py:213-215 (`sys.exit(1)` on no NIFs). CORRECTIONS: (1) Severity framing for verify_zero_weight_bones is overstated: memory project_zeroweight_bone_class.md (2026-08-25) demoted the absolute census to an OBSERVATION ('as a gate it can only ever say FAIL', pack carries ~1136) and made zero_weight_pair_ab.py the gate, which has a floor (settled entry, :234). The vacuous pass still matters (the index line still orders the run) but the CTD-gate weight belongs to pair_ab. (2) The proof's trigger is wrong for the two verify_* tools: they resolve `mods_root / CBBE2UBE_OUT_MOD / meshes / !UBE` (:49,:61) - an EMPTY mod dir returns 1 at :63 `not root.is_dir()`. The vacuous case needs `meshes/!UBE` present with zero .nif (partially purged output, wrong OUT_MOD name that happens to have the dir); the out_dir-with-`meshes` trap from feedback_single_batch_parity is not the route for these two. (3) `_DEFAULT_PIECES` fallback is a deliberate mod-agnostic design (golden_output.py:41 'so this file stays mod-agnostic'); the fix is a loud print + a compared-count floor + a manifest-vs-PIECES label mismatch warning, not removal of the fallback. COUNT METHOD: read each of the six verdict lines and population builds directly; did not recount the full 69-tool complement (TOOL_MAP shows 13 'yes' of 69 rows by keyword; settled records 20 real floors by branch read).

**Fix.** Add `_census_common.require_population(items, what, min_n=1)` printing the standard 'measured NOTHING -- 0/0 is not a pass' line and `raise SystemExit(3)`; call it after the population build in verify_zero_weight_bones (:69), verify_weight_invariant (:72), fit_audit (:312), scan_morph_issues (:260), physics_cloth_health (replace :175 `return 0` with exit 3 - keep the accounting print). golden_output: `_load_pieces` prints which set it loaded; check() counts `compared`, exits 2 on 0, and warns when `man["pieces"]` has labels not in PIECES (signature of a swapped set); keep the `_DEFAULT_PIECES` fallback (mod-agnostic by design, :41). Fix `_has_floor` so a probe must hit a non-docstring line (or use the AST), and add the negative test.

**Proof it landed.** Tools only, `git diff --stat src/` empty. For the two verify_* tools create `<mods_root>/<OUT_MOD>/meshes/!UBE/` with NO .nif (an empty mod dir alone exits 1 today at :63) and assert exit 3 (today 0 with 'clean -- every bone...'). For fit_audit/scan_morph_issues/physics_cloth_health pass `--output` at a dir whose `meshes/` exists and is empty, assert exit != 0 (today 0). Golden: move golden/pieces.json aside on a machine with a custom baseline, run `check`, assert refusal (today PASS over 0 comparisons). Add to tests/test_tool_map.py a fixture tool whose only 'empty set' is in its docstring and assert `floor` is False (today golden_output.py is reported yes on exactly that).

**Prior art.** known-open: feedback_census_population.md ('0/0 is not a pass'); project_scratchpad_tooling.md:66-69 (floor share is a lower bound, tool_audit only covers source-parsing tools - confirmed at tool_audit.py:172 `if reads_source(text)`); feedback_method_traps.md L339-341 (golden pieces.json fallback reports 6/6); project_zeroweight_bone_class.md (absolute zero-weight census demoted to observation 2026-08-25; delta gate zero_weight_pair_ab has a floor).

### F005 [HIGH] PRECONDITION A: six structure guards are coupled to the single filename nif_convert.py; a moved pass is dropped by five of them without any test failing, and the sibling blindness is already live for fit_metrics.minimum_push
`scripts/pass_map.py:174` · effort M · risk none · lens monolith-seams · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=new, fix=reframe, confidence=high

**Claim.** Six guards the project built to stop exactly the 'wired into one path / swallowed silently' classes are all coupled to one filename. After any extraction: the pass-map tables lose every row whose pass is now imported (the guard column too, since `flag_consts` reads only this file); the parity walker stops checking every SHARED pass that moved (only the 9 KNOWN_PHASE2_ONLY and 12 NOT_A_FIT_PASS names fail loudly via their 'no longer exists' asserts); the three swallow ratchets stop scanning moved handlers. The floors catch only a collapse, not a 30% shrink. This is the class docs/worklog/AUDIT_MAIN_LAYOUT.md already paid for: 'Moving those 35 files silently removed 29 tests, and the suite stayed green.' It must be fixed BEFORE the first move or the split silently disarms the guards the rest of this lens relies on.

**Evidence.** scripts/pass_map.py L62 `SRC = REPO / "src" / "nif_convert.py"`; L174 `if lbl is None and name not in defs:` / `continue` (a call to a name not DEFINED in this file is dropped from the table); L208 `if len(defs) < 100 or len(consts) < 100:` (the only floor; 308 defs today). tests/test_convert_path_parity.py L68-69 `_module_ast()` -> `ast.parse(Path(inspect.getfile(nc)).read_text(...))`, L73 `top = {n.name: n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef)}` (a moved pass leaves BOTH reachable sets; MIN_PASSES = 20). tests/test_no_silent_pass_failures.py L56 `ast.parse(Path(inspect.getfile(nc))...)` (floor: >=40 PASS_HINTS name matches). tests/test_no_silent_save.py L46 `Path(inspect.getsourcefile(nc))` (floor >=12 handlers). tests/test_deterministic_output.py L427 and L448 `handlers = _all_silent_handlers(nc.__file__)` (floor >=60).

**Verifier.** Every one of the six cited guard sites is coupled to nif_convert.py exactly as claimed, and the floors are as stated (pass_map 100/100; parity MIN_PASSES=20; test_no_silent_pass_failures L159 >=40; test_no_silent_save L75 >=12, L96 >=7; test_deterministic_output L427/L448 >=60). Re-counted: 30,333 lines, 308 module-level defs (pass_map.module_level_defs), 309 flag/knob constants (pass_map.flag_consts), 48 topic banners (regex `^# *[-=#]{3,}.*[A-Za-z].*[-=#]{3,} *$`) vs the header's '~28k lines with 41 topic banners' (L21-22) -- the doc drift is real. AUDIT_MAIN_LAYOUT.md L71 quote verified verbatim. The blindness is ALREADY LIVE, not hypothetical: `fit_metrics.minimum_push(` is called at nif_convert.py L29331 inside convert_nif_phase2 (def L27997) -- a producer pass on the body-swap path -- and docs/PASS_MAP.md has 0 occurrences of `minimum_push` or `fit_metrics`; the parity `_call_graph` builds `top` from `tree.body` FunctionDefs only, so it cannot see it either. Two corrections: (1) 'most go SILENT' overstates pass_map -- tests/test_pass_map.py::test_pass_map_is_current pins the WHOLE generated doc, so a moved pass fails that test loudly; what is silent is the sanctioned response (regenerate) because no per-entry row floor exists (grep of test_pass_map.py for row/len: nothing), i.e. the header's own 'small, tidy, wrong map' class. The parity walker (both reachable sets lose the pass, `only_a`/`extra` stay empty, L208-238) and the three ratchets are silent as claimed. (2) pass_map's single-module scope is a DOCUMENTED deliberate limit (scripts/pass_map.py L48-50: 'Only functions defined in THIS module... sibling modules are out of scope on purpose -- the question is which of OUR passes run'); the finding is new in that this rationale already fails for minimum_push and fails wholesale once OUR passes live in siblings, and in that the other five guards carry no such documented limit. No split attempt exists in the 577-commit history (no commit matches split/extract-module), so 'not recorded as a split precondition' holds.

**Fix.** As proposed, with two changes: item (4) should be a per-entry MINIMUM row floor in tests/test_pass_map.py (the doc pin already fails on any change; what is missing is a floor that a regeneration cannot lower without editing the test), and a parity pass-set MINIMUM per entry, not just MIN_PASSES=20; add (6) include `src/fit_metrics.py` in the declared module list now, so `minimum_push` gains a row and a parity entry before any move -- it is the one existing sibling-module pass on a production path.

**Proof it landed.** Tooling only; `python scripts/pass_map.py --check` exit 0 and each guard-the-guard mutation test still fires after widening. Additionally assert PASS_MAP.md gains a `minimum_push` row under convert_nif_phase2 once fit_metrics is in the list (the existing sibling case proves cross-file visibility without a throwaway worktree). ### F009 [HIGH] The exe carries no build identity: '1.3' has shipped on 12 distinct builds (8 committed + 4 uncommitted), the deployed exe is 4 builds past the last committed blob, and the three hand-kept CRC records disagree (946DB86E / E14551C6 / 11348715)

**Claim.** Every in-game sighting is scored against an exe CRC that only exists in hand-maintained notes, and those notes have already drifted three ways within four days. The only identification the exe emits is auto_convert.py:914 `_echo_active_experiment_flags` (env flags only; line 2 of the deployed run log begins `active flags (3):`), the GUI title `v{_app_version}` (gui.py:429) and `CBBE-to-UBE auto-conversion report (v{_app_version})` (auto_convert.py:779); write_conversion_summary (2204-2301) prints no version at all. Measured today: deployed exe = dist exe (sha256 5d109719…, CRC32 11348715, 8,574,748 b, mtime Aug 28 15:34) but HEAD's tracked blob is 7f825ff6… (CRC32 A3E8681B, 08-25) — two builds ahead of the last dist commit and in no commit. IN_GAME_BUGS.md:24 `exe DEPLOYED CRC32 946DB86E, 8,573,998 b` (header 're-verified 2026-08-26'); MEMORY.md:2 `exe E14551C6`; project_current_state.md 08-29 says 11348715.

**Verifier.** Substance confirmed by independent re-derivation; two counts corrected. src/version.py:27 `__version__ = "1.3"`, set by 9e435e8 'Release 1.3' (08-19) and untouched since; its own docstring (:20-22) promises 'any bug report is traceable to the exact build' -- false for every build since. Tags: v1.3-alpha only, no v1.3; `gh release list`: 1.3-alpha (Pre-release), 1.2 (Latest). CHANGELOG.md:3 `## Unreleased`, :223 `## 1.3 -- 2026-08-19`. RE-COUNT of rebuilds carrying '1.3': git log on dist/CBBEtoUBE/CBBEtoUBE.exe after fe384c4 (the 1.3 binary) lists 8 commits, each a distinct blob (3b714ed 08-19, 80fd39c, 02e9ba5, e8a9133, 14f664b, a871e94, 87d14e3, 0586500 08-25), plus 4 uncommitted deploys in project_current_state.md (946DB86E :344, E14551C6 :314, 8BAF0B9D :192, 11348715 :125) = 12 exactly; '>=12' holds. Hashes re-derived with hashlib/zlib: deployed == dist = sha256 5d109719 / CRC32 11348715 / 8,574,748 b (mtime Aug 28 15:34); HEAD (= testing) blob 7f825ff6 / A3E8681B / 8,571,399; origin/testing 1522194a / 303F7BAE; main 353acdcb / CB35E8CF. CORRECTION: the deployed exe is FOUR builds past the last committed blob (0586500, 08-25), not two. Three-way drift confirmed verbatim: IN_GAME_BUGS.md:24 `exe DEPLOYED CRC32 946DB86E, 8,573,998 b` under 're-verified 2026-08-26'; MEMORY.md:2 `exe E14551C6`; project_current_state.md:13 `DEPLOYED **11348715**`; and a fourth stale copy inside current_state's own frontmatter (:3 'exe **946DB86E** BUILT + DEPLOYED'). What the exe emits: log line 2 is `active flags (3): ...` (auto_convert.py:932-937), the title `v{_app_version}` (gui.py:429), the report header (auto_convert.py:779); the finding MISSED that report_template.build_report / issue_url also take _app_version (gui.py:2103,2134; report_template.py:81-92) -- but every one of these carries the same opaque '1.3', so no surface identifies the build. write_conversion_summary (2204-2301) has no version/build token (grep). No build-stamp mechanism exists anywhere (grep _build_info/rev-parse/hashlib(sys.executable) over src, scripts, spec, tests: only scripts/golden_output.py:187 uses `git rev-parse` for the dev golden files). Prior art: project_converter_deploy_path.md's --incremental latent-bug note already proposes 'a build stamp' for a different symptom (auto_convert.py:317-319 falls back to the exe mtime when frozen) -- related, not the same defect; e8a9133 'Make the converter report on itself' added the flag echo, not a build id. Not recorded as a defect (memory, CHANGELOG, docs/worklog, IN_GAME_BUGS archive, phase1_settled: the settled 'packaging-deploy' entry only states deployed==dist today). REFRAME: (a) 'bump __version__ on every deployed build' contradicts version.py's stated policy (bump per release) and is unnecessary once a stamp exists -- keep the release version, add the build stamp; (b) generating src/_build_info.py inside the tree at build time makes the tree dirty and trips its own `git status --porcelain src/` check unless the file is gitignored and excluded from that check (or the stamp is passed via the spec's datas/env instead); (c) the porcelain check should cover CBBEtoUBE.spec and cbbe_to_ube_main.py too; (d) the 'never re-derivable from a commit' clause depends on the sibling non-reproducibility finding, which I did not verify -- the stamp's value does not depend on it. Risk 'none' is honest: printing/stamping touches no vertex, weight, bone, XML byte or plugin record; the exe bytes change, so the proof's convert-parity compare via exe_parity_convert.py before any in-game scoring is the right gate. Rule 8 clean.

**Fix.** Keep the per-release __version__ policy; add a build stamp: build_exe.ps1 captures `git rev-parse --short HEAD`, `+dirty` if `git status --porcelain src/ CBBEtoUBE.spec cbbe_to_ube_main.py` is non-empty (with the generated stamp file itself gitignored and excluded), UTC time and PyInstaller version, delivered either as a gitignored src/_build_info.py or via the spec's datas; at startup print one line `build <sha>[+dirty] <utc> exe-sha256 <8>` (hash sys.executable when frozen) right after the flag echo in _echo_active_experiment_flags; stamp the same string into the report header (:779), conversion_summary.txt, conversion_report.json and report_template.build_report; make the IN_GAME_BUGS header and current_state quote the printed stamp instead of a hand CRC.

**Proof it landed.** Run the rebuilt deployed exe headless (`CBBE2UBE_NO_PAUSE=1 ... scan`, no conversion) and grep CBBEtoUBE_last_run.log for the build line; assert its sha256 prefix equals hashlib.sha256(exe) and its git sha equals the blob's commit; extend tests/test_gui_reporting_surface.py:246 so build_report includes the stamp. Then convert-compare the rebuilt exe against 11348715 with scripts/analysis/exe_parity_convert.py (vertex compare, not bytes) before it is scored in game.

**Prior art.** new -- project_converter_deploy_path.md floats 'a build stamp' only as a future fix for the frozen --incremental floor; no record names the missing build id as a defect.

### F015 [HIGH] Voiced caller, mute callee: the three smoothing helpers swallow their whole body and return the input, so the caller-side `_note_pass_failure` wrappers can never fire -- and 7 of their 10 call sites have no wrapper at all
`src/nif_convert.py:4825` · effort S · risk vert-neutral · lens swallowed-failures · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=new, fix=sound, confidence=high

**Claim.** The 08-22 census fixed the inverse shape (a mute OUTER around a voiced INNER). This is the mirror: the recorder is on the outside and the callee eats everything first, so the label exists in the code, the tests that grep for it pass, and a real failure still produces the exact 'unsmoothed = nothing qualified' output. `_smooth_push_field` is the anti-poke feather (SNUGNESS push #2); `_smooth_warp_grooves` is the groove pass default ON (L2596 `GROOVE_SMOOTH_ENABLED = not _flag(..., False)`).

Probe over all 84 distinct `_note_pass_failure` labels found exactly these 3 label-named functions whose top-level silent broad handler covers >=50% of the function.

**Verifier.** CONFIRMED: all three callees swallow their whole working body and return the input, so the caller-side `_note_pass_failure` wrappers are unreachable for anything inside the try. `_smooth_push_field` L4794-4825: L4801 docstring 'Returns `push` unchanged on any failure', L4825-4826 `except Exception: return push`; the only statement outside its try is L4802 `iters = _reach_iters(...)`, and `_reach_iters` (L4763-4792) is itself gated `if not SMOOTH_REACH or verts is None: return iters` (SMOOTH_REACH L4607 default False) and ends `except Exception: return iters`, so nothing can escape to the wrapper. `_smooth_vertex_field` L26606-26639 same structure, L26639 `return vec`. `_smooth_warp_grooves` L2637-2771: L2675 `try:` ... L2770 `except Exception:` / L2771 `return warped`. Coverage re-counted by AST (scratchpad/v_f014_probe.py): 0.73 / 0.69 / 0.72 of the function span -- matches the finding's 24/33, 24/35, 97/135. Label-vs-function probe: exactly 3 label-named functions carry a top-level silent broad handler covering >=50% (all other 23 hits are early-guard handlers at 1-12%) -- the finding's '3' is confirmed. NUMBER CORRECTION: distinct `_note_pass_failure` literal labels = 129 by AST (52 name a module function), not 84; not load-bearing. SCOPE CORRECTION (strengthens it): the callers cited are a subset. `_smooth_push_field` has 3 call sites: L3451 (`warp_armor_by_body_delta`, WARP_STANDOFF_SMOOTH_ENABLED default ON, NO wrapper), L5412 (`clear_armor_outside_body` -- this is the anti-poke feather, gated ANTIPOKE_SMOOTH_ENABLED L1611 default False with GUI row L216 default=False, NO wrapper), L5559 (`_inflate_cloth_over_bust_butt`, the softcloth inflate, smooth_iters=4 default, wrapped with `_note_pass_failure("_smooth_push_field")` L5562). So the finding's 'SNUGNESS push #2 (anti-poke)' attribution belongs to L5412, which is OFF by default; the ON-by-default reachers are the warp standoff feather (L3451) and the softcloth inflate (L5559). `_smooth_vertex_field` has 5 call sites (L3848 rebury, L12101 self-intersection relax, L26795 layer order, L27434/27436 ride feather) -- only two have the label wrapper. `_smooth_warp_grooves`: phase-2 L28896 wrapped, phase-1 L7084 unwrapped inside the whole-fit try (comment L7079-7083 confirmed), GROOVE_SMOOTH_ENABLED L2596 default ON. Putting the voice inside the callee therefore covers all 10 call sites at once, which the caller-side design never can. Prior art: the 08-22 census (IN_GAME_BUGS.md L3398-3408) and the test docstring L25-31 cover only the mute-OUTER/voiced-INNER shape; commit 1580651 (07-31) explicitly says 'the handler still swallows, it just speaks first' about the caller wrappers -- the callee side was never examined; memory feedback_method_traps.md L24 records the outer class only; phase1_settled.json mentions `_smooth_warp_grooves` only as a pass-contract note. Fix sound and vert-neutral: handler-only edit, returns unchanged, `_note_pass_failure` never raises; only summary text changes, and only when a failure was already being eaten.

**Fix.** Voice inside each callee: `except Exception as _e: _note_pass_failure("_smooth_push_field", _e); return push` (L4825), same for `_smooth_vertex_field` (L26639, `return vec`) and `_smooth_warp_grooves` (L2770, `return warped`). This also covers the 7 unwrapped call sites (L3451, L5412, L3848, L27434, L27436, L7084 and the phase-1 groove call) that the caller-side design misses. Keep the caller wrappers (harmless). Add the structural rule to the guard test: a function whose name is a `_note_pass_failure` label may not contain a top-level silent broad handler spanning >=50% of its body. Correct the write-up: the anti-poke feather (L5412, ANTIPOKE_SMOOTH) is OFF by default; the default-ON reachers are the warp standoff feather (L3451) and the softcloth inflate (L5559).

**Proof it landed.** Handler-only edit, returns unchanged; confirm by rule 5: single-harness `exe_parity_convert.py` A/B, PYTHONHASHSEED=1, vertex-identical. Raise-injection: monkeypatch `scipy.sparse.coo_matrix` (inside the try, reached only when `push.any()`) to raise, call `_inflate_cloth_over_bust_butt` with tris on a synthetic bust patch, assert `"PASS FAILED _smooth_push_field"` in `nc._piece_pass_failures()` -- fails on the current tree because the callee returns `push` before the wrapper sees anything. Note `_reach_iters` is NOT a usable injection point: it is outside the try but swallows its own failures and is gated off by SMOOTH_REACH=False.

**Prior art.** New. The 08-22 census and tests/test_no_silent_pass_failures.py L25-31 record only the inverse (mute OUTER around voiced INNER); commit 1580651 added the caller-side wrappers and states the handler 'still swallows, it just speaks first' -- the callee was not examined.

`src/nif_convert.py:23311` · effort S · risk vert-neutral · lens swallowed-failures · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=new, fix=sound, confidence=high

**Claim.** An effect-shader shape (a glowing overlay on an armour) whose transplant raises ships as a white quad, and a lighting-shader shape whose `write_properties()` raises ships with default specular/rim/emissive values. **Evidence.** L23311-23312 `    except Exception:\n        pass` closes the try opened at L23237 (`src_shader = src_shape.shader`) that contains the BSEffectShaderProperty branch: L23273-23282 `eff = dst_nif.add_block(src_shader.name or "", eff_buf, parent=new_shape)` / `new_shape.properties.shaderPropertyID = eff.id` under its own inner `except Exception: pass` (L23281-23282), and the lighting-shader branch's `new_shader.write_properties()` under `except Exception: pass` (L23305-23306). The block's own comment L23253-23259 states the consequence: 'createShapeFromData only ever makes a BSLightingShaderProperty ... renders the bare (pale) overlay texture = WHITE, no glow'. `_copy_shape` runs for every shape on both paths (rule-6 check: it is in PASS_HINTS as a CALL, but this try is INSIDE it and calls only `add_block`/`_transplant_effect_controller`/`write_properties`, none hinted).

**Verifier.** Confirmed at the cited lines and stronger than stated. L23238-23312 in `_copy_shape` is one try whose outer handler is `except Exception:\n        pass` (L23311-23312); inside it the effect-shader transplant `eff = dst_nif.add_block(src_shader.name or "", eff_buf, parent=new_shape)` / `new_shape.properties.shaderPropertyID = eff.id` (L23273-23280) sits under its own mute handler (L23281-23282), and the lighting branch's `new_shader.write_properties()` under another (L23305-23306). The ratchet in tests/test_no_silent_pass_failures.py cannot see any of them: reproduced its AST walk read-only against src/nif_convert.py -> 0 reported handlers, and the try's call set is {_pynifly, _transplant_effect_controller, add_block, from_buffer_copy, getattr, hasattr, setattr, type, write_properties}, none matching PASS_HINTS (which includes `_copy_shape` as a CALL, i.e. only the callers of _copy_shape are guarded, not this try). File-wide there are 225 broad+silent handlers; this one is not in the 08-22 census. Consequence is the code's own comment L23253-23259 and memory project_effect_shader_glow.md: a failed transplant ships the shape with the default BSLightingShaderProperty = white, no glow. Two under-stated consequences: (a) the controller handler L23276-23280 silently sets `eff_buf.controllerID = _pyn.NODEID_NONE` -> a glow that ships STATIC, also unreported; (b) a failed transplant leaves a lighting shader on the OUTPUT shape, so the output-side skip gates `_shape_has_effect_shader(s) or _is_fx_overlay_name(s.name)` (L12486, L12753, L15724) degrade to the name heuristic alone (`"glow" in nm or ":fx" in nm or nm.endswith("fx")`, L12472) -- a glow shape not so named is then grafted/re-saved, the 2fff3cc/b55587a equip-CTD class. No post-write check exists: `bufType` appears at only 2 sites in nif_convert.py (L12507 gate helper, L23245 this transplant), both pre-write; `_repair_effect_shader_shape_controllers` L9144-9175 only resets controllerID==shaderPropertyID. `_note_pass_failure` (L5744) feeds `_PASS_FAILURES_THIS_PIECE` -> `reason` (L7791, L30320) -> conversion_summary/report, so voicing does reach the operator. 76-line count checks (L23237-23312). Reach: `_copy_shape` called at L7414/7419 (copy path) and L25023/25612/29979 (phase 2).

**Fix.** Voice three levels, all with the shape name: outer L23311 `_note_pass_failure(f"_copy_shape/shader:{src_shape.name}", _e)`; the add_block/shaderPropertyID handler L23281 `_note_pass_failure(f"_copy_shape/effect-shader:{src_shape.name}", _e)`; the controller handler L23276 `_note_pass_failure(f"_copy_shape/effect-controller:{src_shape.name}", _e)` before the NODEID_NONE fallback (a static glow is a visible degrade too). Leave the per-field setattr handler L23301-23302 (narrow TypeError/AttributeError) alone. Do NOT change the fallback behaviour itself (keep the shape; keep static-glow fallback) -- reporting only.

**Proof it landed.** Handler-only edit on the exception path; no vertex/weight/bone/XML/plugin byte can change (only report text). Parity: single-harness `exe_parity_convert.py` A/B (rule 5, PYTHONHASHSEED=1 for source runs). Raise-injection: tests/synthetic_nif.py `build_effect_shader_nif` already builds a real BSEffectShaderProperty + controller chain (per project_effect_shader_glow.md); monkeypatch `dst_nif.add_block` to raise once, call `_copy_shape`, assert `_piece_pass_failures()` contains `_copy_shape/effect-shader:` and the shape still exists; second case monkeypatch `nc._transplant_effect_controller` to raise, assert `/effect-controller:` recorded and `controllerID == NODEID_NONE`.

**Prior art.** New. The transplant design (project_effect_shader_glow.md, DESIGN.md 'Effect-shader glow overlays') documents WHAT is transplanted but never accepted a silent failure; the 2026-08-22 silent-handler census (tests/test_no_silent_pass_failures.py) is PASS_HINTS-scoped and this try's calls are unhinted, so it was never counted.

### F019 [HIGH] Inside `_finalize_hdt_physics` the authored-XML copy is `except Exception: pass` and the resulting `return False` is discarded: the piece ships with an UNHARDENED XML and no collider re-import, and the source-of-truth binding hides it from every diagnostic
