### F073 [MEDIUM] _finalize_hdt_physics is the producer for three on-disk repair passes (shape-order restore, TRI refresh, XML split repoint), each a full NIF reload+save; the producer-side ordering fixes are not recorded as considered
`src/nif_convert.py:8731` · effort M · risk behaviour · lens pass-contract · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=known-accepted, fix=reframe, confidence=medium

**Claim.** Rule-1 grade: three CLEANUPs for one PRODUCER's ordering, and the docstring of the first proves the producer-side form is available (route 1 was fixed at the source). Each repair is also a re-save — the DESIGN doc's own "a pass that reports success can still be DISCARDED" class, and the site where a lost write hides (`test_no_silent_save.py` exists precisely because seven such saves once failed silently). Cost is time on every piece plus three places for a save to be lost; the shape-order repair also had to be invented after an in-game bug (BUG-09) that a producer fix would have prevented.

**Evidence.** 8731 `_restore_authored_shape_order` docstring: "TWO ROUTES PERMUTED THE SHAPES, and this pass only has to repair the second: 1. ... Fixed structurally by moving the injection after pass 2 -- no repair needed ... 2. `_finalize_hdt_physics` DROPS textureless collision proxies during the copy and re-appends them `sorted()` at the END. That is this pass's job". 24754: `missing = sorted(n for n in col_names if n not in present)` — the comment gives determinism as the only reason for the order. 9536 `_refresh_armor_tri_after_reimport`: "Only afterwards does `_finalize_hdt_physics` re-import them ... Final NIF has them; the TRI never does"; 7739: "the TRI was written before _finalize_hdt_physics restored the textureless proxies". 17435 `_split_bust_collider_xml`: "runs AFTER _finalize_hdt_physics -- which OVERWRITES the on-disk XML with the authored source copy, silently undoing any earlier rewrite (that trap restored the exact configuration that tore breasts off)". PIPELINE §2d: each on-disk pass "re-loads and re-saves the NIF".

**Verifier.** Docstrings and code match the quotes: 8731-8740 `_restore_authored_shape_order` ('TWO ROUTES PERMUTED THE SHAPES'), 24754 `missing = sorted(n for n in col_names if n not in present)` with determinism as the only stated reason, 9536 `_refresh_armor_tri_after_reimport`, 7737-7739, 17435-17440 `_split_bust_collider_xml` 'runs AFTER _finalize_hdt_physics -- which OVERWRITES the on-disk XML'. Commit 617640f (`git log -S_restore_authored_shape_order`) gives the reason the repair form was chosen: 'from the shared tail so both convert paths get it' and 'a no-op when the order already matches' — a recorded design choice, so known-accepted for (a); the finding adds (b) and (c), which appear nowhere, so keep. RISK is misclassified: (b) moves TRI generation and (c) changes who writes the physics XML, so .tri bytes and XML bytes CAN change — by the owner's definition that is not vert-neutral; call it 'behaviour' with the proof already listed (tri bytes identical, BODYTRI+HDT pointers diffed).

**Fix.** (a) In `_finalize_hdt_physics` (24741-24790), re-import each dropped proxy at its AUTHORED index (the source NIF is an argument; `_restore_authored_shape_order` already computes the target order) so the restore pass becomes a 0-change ratchet. (b) Move `_collect_tri_inputs`/TRI generation (7633 / 30196) after `_finalize_physics_and_motion_match`, or write the TRI once from the finalized NIF, so `_refresh_armor_tri_after_reimport` returns False everywhere — first confirming PIPELINE §2d's "last among the extra-data writers" constraint concerns the BODYTRI extra-data block, not the .tri file. (c) Have the finalize write the split decl itself (it already knows the clone exists, pass 1 ran before it).

**Proof it landed.** Convert-based comparison, not bytes: for a pack sample, the three repairs must each report 0 (shapes reordered 0, TRI rewritten False, decls rewritten 0 — via `scripts/analysis/change_attribution.py` on the run report); final NIF shape ORDER identical to today's output; `.tri` bytes identical; BODYTRI + HDT pointers diffed identical (the deploy gate); `node_parity.py` 0 verts / 0 weight rows / 0 node translations differing; BUG-09 alt-texture index check unchanged; `tests/test_no_silent_save.py`.

**Prior art.** commit 617640f: repair kept 'from the shared tail so both convert paths get it'; (b)/(c) unrecorded

### F078 [MEDIUM] GUI-process probes read os.environ, not the settings state: preflight ignores the Paths-tab UBE body, the mod list ignores the vanilla-sweep toggle, and a non-existent path override is silently ignored at run time
`src/nif_convert.py:2038` · effort S · risk behaviour · lens settings-plumbing · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=new, fix=sound, confidence=high

**Claim.** 'Check setup' can FAIL ("No BodySlide-built UBE body found ... or set CBBE2UBE_UBE_BODY") for a user whose Paths-tab override is valid, or pass on auto-discovery while the override they typed points nowhere -- and the run then silently converts against the auto-discovered body instead of the one they chose, with no warning anywhere. With 'Convert vanilla armor' unticked the Select-mods list still offers 'vanilla' (only the exclusions dialog filters it, gui.py:1879-1883); ticking it sends `--only-mods vanilla` plus NO_VANILLA_SWEEP=1 to the child, which then converts nothing. Controls that lie about the run they describe.

**Evidence.** _find_ube_femalebody: `bare = os.environ.get("CBBE2UBE_UBE_BODY")` (2038) ... `if cand.is_file(): ... return cand` (2043-2044) else falls through to discovery with no message; the weight-specific override (2031-2034) and overlay_transfer.find_texconv (79-81 `if env and Path(env).is_file(): found = Path(env)`) behave the same. preflight._probe_ube_body (56-62) calls that function with the GUI process's environment, and gui.py runs it as `checks = pf.run_checks(want_overlays=scope[0], want_overlay_copy=scope[1])` (1957) without applying settings. gui.py `_settings_set` (810-816) writes state["settings"] and save_values only -- the only os.environ write in gui.py is `CBBE2UBE_NO_PAUSE` (2569); apply_env is used solely to build the child's env (2486). auto_convert.list_convertible_mods appends the "vanilla" pseudo-source when `os.environ.get("CBBE2UBE_NO_VANILLA_SWEEP", "") != "1"` (4900-4906), and _find_armor_mod_dirs keys its memo on the same env with the comment "a mid-process toggle returns a stale list" (4620-4625) -- a toggle that never reaches the env.

**Verifier.** Confirmed. `_find_ube_femalebody` reads `os.environ.get("CBBE2UBE_UBE_BODY")` (nif_convert.py:2038) and falls through silently when `cand.is_file()` is False (2043-2044); preflight._probe_ube_body (56-62) calls it in the GUI process; gui.py runs `pf.run_checks(...)` at 1957 and 2164 without applying settings, and the only os.environ write in gui.py is CBBE2UBE_NO_PAUSE at 2569 -- apply_env is used only for the child env (2486). list_convertible_mods appends 'vanilla' on the env var (auto_convert.py:4900-4904); the GUI's Select-mods list at 635 uses that lister unfiltered while only the exclusions dialog filters vanilla (1879-1883), and 2373-2375 emits `--only-mods <name>` for any ticked entry. Memory project_comment_audit_2026_08_17.md:33 notes UBE_BODY 'IS read bare' but not the GUI/preflight disagreement. Risk 'behaviour' is honest (GUI-side behaviour changes; child unchanged pending the proof it names).

**Fix.** In the GUI process, keep os.environ in sync with the registry: call `gui_settings.apply_env(state["settings"], base_env=dict(os.environ))` and write the result into os.environ at load and inside `_settings_set` (registry vars authoritative, as the child already treats them; the GUI never converts, and the three readers involved -- UBE_BODY, TEXCONV, NO_VANILLA_SWEEP -- are call-time reads per the AST census, so import-time constants are irrelevant here). Separately, make a set-but-missing path override LOUD: in _find_ube_femalebody/_find_ube_shapedata/find_texconv, when the env var is set and `is_file()` is False, print a WARN and record it via _note_pass_failure-style channel so it reaches the report (ties to the run_config finding).

**Proof it landed.** GUI/preflight-only behaviour; the conversion child is unchanged, which must still be shown by a convert-based vertex comparison of one piece before/after (same harness, both arms) rather than asserted. Tests: with settings ube_body pointing at a tmp file, `pf.run_checks()` in-process reports that path for the ubebody check; with vanilla_sweep=False, list_convertible_mods() omits 'vanilla'; with CBBE2UBE_UBE_BODY set to a non-existent path, _find_ube_femalebody emits the warning (capsys) and the piece result carries it.

**Prior art.** not found in CHANGELOG, IN_GAME_BUGS, docs or memory.

### F080 [MEDIUM] Skeleton caches memoise an EMPTY result for the process lifetime and nothing records which skeleton a run resolved, although converter output is skeleton-dependent
`src/nif_convert.py:24262` · effort S · risk behaviour · lens settings-plumbing · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=known-open, fix=reframe, confidence=high

**Claim.** In production the parent exports paths (5183) before its first nif_convert body call (5223) and workers inherit the env before the pool exists (2854 vs 2900/2913 -- verified by a real spawn probe), so the empty-skeleton lock-in is a harness/first-caller trap today (memory: postrun_audit check B). But every bone classification, anchor seeding and FSMP pruning decision depends on the installed skeleton, and no artefact records which file (or that none) was used -- so two users' packs, or a harness and the exe, can differ silently with no way to tell from the report.

**Evidence.** _actor_skeleton_bone_names: `_SKELETON_BONES_CACHE = names` / `return names` (24262-24263) after `for pat in (...): p = _glob_first_in_mods(pat)` -- assigned unconditionally, empty set included; _actor_skeleton_bone_parents likewise `_SKELETON_PARENTS_CACHE = out` (24306). _glob_first_in_mods returns None when `_paths.mods_root()` is None (25326-25328), and unlike the positives-only `_GLOB_FIRST_MEMO[memo_key]` keyed on `(pattern, name_substrs, str(root))` (25332, 25348) the skeleton caches carry no root in their key and no 'loaded' flag. _actor_can_resolve_bone then `if not skel: return _is_skeleton_bone(name)` (10070-10071) -- a silent fallback to the name heuristic. Cache census: 28 module-global caches; _BODY_DISCOVERY_CACHE (1955) is keyed by weight string only (`ck = f"cbbe{weight}"`, 1990), _BODY_CONFORM/LEG_DETAIL/JIGGLE_REF/SELFINT caches by `weight` only; none is ever cleared except _HDT_XML_TEXT_CACHE (25135).

**Verifier.** Confirmed. `_SKELETON_BONES_CACHE = names` (24262) and `_SKELETON_PARENTS_CACHE = out` (24306) are assigned unconditionally after the loop, so an empty result (mods_root None -> `_glob_first_in_mods` returns None at 25326-25328) is memoised for the process; unlike `_GLOB_FIRST_MEMO` the key carries no root. `_actor_can_resolve_bone` falls back to `_is_skeleton_bone(name)` on empty (10070-10071) -- memory project_current_state.md:1622-1624 audited that fallback as SOUND by design, and feedback_method_traps records the first-caller cache trap, so the lock-in is known; the finding's new content is the missing report surface (which skeleton was used). Risk reframe: 'vert-neutral' holds only for the production order (env exported before first call); in any process where the first call precedes mods_root export, un-memoising the empty set CHANGES bone classification/anchor/pruning results -- that is the point of the fix, so classify as 'behaviour' with the convert-based proof it already names.

**Fix.** Do not memoise an empty skeleton (return the empty set without caching, or key the cache on str(mods_root) like _GLOB_FIRST_MEMO); print one main-process line 'skeleton: <path> (<N> bones)' from _cmd_auto after export_to_env, and put path + bone count into the report's run_config. Consider the same 'no-negative-memo' rule for _BODY_DISCOVERY_CACHE's None entries, which also lock in a failed discovery for the process.

**Proof it landed.** As stated (convert-based vertex + node-translation compare in one harness, PYTHONHASHSEED=1) -- required precisely because the change is not neutral when the first caller precedes mods_root export.

**Prior art.** memory feedback_method_traps (first-caller cache) and project_current_state.md:1622-1624 (empty-skeleton fallback audited as sound); the report surface is new.

### F082 [MEDIUM] First-party and scipy imports are guarded by `except Exception` with a silent default return — the exact 'one missing import made an entire pass a silent no-op' trap, on the generated-physics-XML path
`src/nif_convert.py:23782` · effort S · risk behaviour · lens swallowed-failures · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=new, fix=sound, confidence=high

**Claim.** A SyntaxError, NameError-at-import or a bad merge in hdt_xml_gen.py makes every piece that needs a generated XML ship physics-free with `return None`, and the run is green. `except Exception` around a bare import or attribute read can only ever be hiding ImportError/AttributeError — anything else it catches is a bug it is concealing.

**Evidence.** L23782-23784 `try:\n    from . import hdt_xml_gen\nexcept Exception:\n    return None` at the top of `_generate_hdt_xml_for_dst` (the generator used when no authored XML exists, docstring L23776-23780); L24439-24441 same idiom `return False` in the body-collider patch. Six more: `from scipy.spatial import cKDTree` under `except Exception: return 0/None` at L14248, L25668, L26695, L26956, L27690, L27940. Probe: 17 silent broad handlers in nif_convert.py guard a body with NO call at all (pure attribute/subscript/import), e.g. L17865/L17996/L18103/L18218 `src_nodes = src_nif.nodes`, L22868 `bw = src_shape.bone_weights`, L16251 `clone.name = name + _BUST_SPLIT_COL_SUFFIX`.

**Verifier.** All cited sites verified: L23782-23784 `try: from . import hdt_xml_gen / except Exception: return None` at the head of `_generate_hdt_xml_for_dst`; L24438-24441 same idiom `return False`; the six `from scipy.spatial import cKDTree` / `except Exception: return 0|None` sites at L14248, 25668, 26695, 26956, 27690, 27940 all present. hdt_xml_gen.py imports only re/dataclasses/pathlib/typing and `.atomic_io` (L44-49), so a top-level import is cycle-free. The class is recorded in feedback_method_traps.md L22-23; these sites are not. Risk 'behaviour' is the honest class (failure path changes; a previously-silent hdt_xml_gen breakage would now surface).

**Fix.** For the two `hdt_xml_gen` imports: import at module top (it is first-party and pure-python: hdt_xml_gen.py imports only re/dataclasses/pathlib/typing/atomic_io) or `except ImportError as _e: _note_pass_failure("hdt_xml_gen/import", _e); return None`. For the scipy imports: `except ImportError` (scipy is a hard dependency of the frozen exe; a failure is a build defect, voice it once). For the 17 no-call bodies: narrow to `(AttributeError, KeyError, TypeError)` as each site warrants.

**Proof it landed.** Narrowing changes only the failure path, but rule 5 still applies: single-harness `exe_parity_convert.py` A/B with PYTHONHASHSEED=1, vertex-identical and HDT-XML-identical (diff BODYTRI + XML against the previous good build per feedback_single_batch_parity). Add a test that stubs `sys.modules['src.hdt_xml_gen']` with a module that raises NameError on attribute access and asserts the import-site now RAISES or records rather than returning None.

**Prior art.** Class: memory/feedback_method_traps.md L22-23 ('One missing import made an entire pass a silent no-op'). Sites: new.

### F097 [MEDIUM] Phase 2 regenerates a stripped-chain authored source with only_loose=True, contradicting the recorded only_loose=False contract the copy path honours
`src/nif_convert.py:7464` · effort S · risk behaviour · lens two-path-drift · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=new, fix=reframe, confidence=medium

**Claim.** For a source whose authored XML references bones the conversion stripped, the copy path re-detects chains and emits a chain XML while phase 2 treats it as 'no physics' and usually emits nothing. Neither comment says the paths differ; `#no-invented-chains` and both softbody gates apply to one path only. The per-path behaviour is undocumented and reads as accidental (the copy path's `only_loose=True` sibling at L7473 shows the author knew the flag).

**Evidence.** Copy L7463-7466: `if _source_hdt_needs_missing_chain_bones(src_path, _dstbones):\n    _regen = _generate_hdt_xml_for_dst(dst_path)` - `only_loose` defaults False (def L23740). Phase 2 L30110-30111 `if _source_hdt_needs_missing_chain_bones(src_path, _dstbones): hdt_xml_path = None` then L30224 `_generate_hdt_xml_for_dst(dst_path, only_loose=True)`. Inside the generator L23990-23993: `if only_loose and not INVENT_CHAINS_FROM_DEAD_BONES: chains = [] else: chains = hdt_xml_gen.detect_physics_chains(all_bones_seen)` and L24018 `if not chains and (only_loose or body_shape_name is None): ... kept rigid (no source physics + no chain bones -> no generated soft-body)`. Both call-site comments claim the same intent ('Regenerate a fresh soft-body XML on standard body bones', L7450-7451 / L30103-30104).

**Verifier.** Lines confirmed: copy L7463-7466 regenerates with only_loose=False (default, def L23740); phase 2 L30110-30111 nulls the XML then L30224 generates with only_loose=True, which under INVENT_CHAINS_FROM_DEAD_BONES=False empties chains (L23990-23993) and the chainless gate (L24018) emits nothing. BUT the owner's recorded decision (IN_GAME_BUGS.md L2937-2942, memory project_fur_auto_smp_explosion) is that only_loose=False regen of an AUTHORED-physics source keeps its chains, pinned by test_an_authored_source_still_gets_its_chain and tests/test_chainless_softbody_gate.py L123-127. So the copy path is the INTENDED behaviour and phase 2 is the odd one out - the finding's 'copy L7464 is the odd one out' is inverted. Note also that on the copy path a stripped-chain source may still end chainless when detect_physics_chains finds nothing in the dst bones (L24018 fires when body_shape_name is None), so the practical divergence is narrower than 'always a chain XML'.

**Fix.** Make phase 2 regenerate at L30110 the way the copy path does (call _generate_hdt_xml_for_dst(dst_path) with only_loose=False and use the result), instead of nulling and falling to the generate-fresh site; document condition->outcome in docs/PIPELINE.md.

**Proof it landed.** XML census by path where the missing-chain check fires (log grep), diff chain blocks; mesh vertex compare must be 0.0; the existing test_an_authored_source_still_gets_its_chain extended to the phase-2 entry; in-game swing verdict on one phase-2 piece.

**Prior art.** IN_GAME_BUGS.md L2937-2942 + tests/test_chainless_softbody_gate.py L123-127 pin only_loose=False for authored sources; the phase-2 nulling at L30110 sidesteps that contract and is unrecorded.

### F100 [MEDIUM] Chain-anchor placement is decided by two different rule sets per path; the GUI 'seed spine anchors' switch reaches phase 2 only
`src/nif_convert.py:28086` · effort M · risk behaviour · lens two-path-drift · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=known-open, fix=sound, confidence=medium

**Claim.** A spine-anchored chain is flat-seeded at its source global on phase 2 (memory project_seed_spine_anchors: confirmed in game) but forces NESTED mode for the whole file on the copy path. The parity guard is satisfied through a branch production never takes, and the GUI switch's label implies pack-wide reach it does not have. Whether the copy-path placement is wrong in game is unknown - it is the two-rule-set structure and the guard's blind spot that are the finding.

**Evidence.** Phase 2 L28086 `_seed_flat_chain_anchors(dst_nif, src_nif)` into the EMPTY dst, with per-anchor rules L18157-18180 (`PER_ANCHOR_ANCHOR_SEED`, `SEED_SPINE_ANCHORS`, skip arm/clavicle/neck/head). The copy path never calls it directly (AST); its only other call site is `_reauthor_nif_fresh` L25010, which convert_nif reaches at L7714-7716 `if not use_rebuild:` - the non-production verbatim branch - and via `_restore_authored_shape_order` L8789 in the shared tail, which seeds from `old` (the already-written copy-path NIF, so it copies whatever placement precreate produced). On the production copy path anchors are created by `_precreate_custom_bone_chains` from `_install_skin` L2352, whose rule is WHOLE-FILE: L18302-18306 `use_nested = (NESTED_CHAIN_ANCHORS or any(_is_upper_body_anchor(a) for a in _heur_anchors) or ...)` with `_UPPER_BODY_ANCHOR_KEYWORDS` including 'spine' (L9755, L9841). gui_settings L941 exposes `CBBE2UBE_NO_SEED_SPINE_ANCHORS`; `_seed_` is in PASS_PREFIXES so the parity guard counts the seed as shared.

**Verifier.** Call-site census confirmed: _seed_flat_chain_anchors is called at L25010 (_reauthor_nif_fresh) and L28086 (phase 2) only; convert_nif reaches _reauthor_nif_fresh directly only under `if not use_rebuild` (L7714, use_rebuild = body_normals_for_fit is not None at L6545) and via _restore_authored_shape_order -> L8789 in the shared tail (_finalize_physics_and_motion_match, L8722), which seeds `new` from `old` = the already-written NIF. Copy-path anchors come from _precreate_custom_bone_chains (L2352) whose whole-file use_nested rule is at L18302-18306. gui_settings L939-942 exposes seed_spine_anchors. Memory project_seed_spine_anchors and project_chain_anchor_seed_mixed_rig do not say which path the verified pieces took. Risk 'behaviour' honest (node globals/XML). Did not re-walk _restore_authored_shape_order's own trigger condition - if it only re-authors when order differs, the copy path's tail seeding is even rarer than stated.

**Fix.** As written; the parity assertion should stop at _reauthor_nif_fresh AND at _restore_authored_shape_order.

**Proof it landed.** As written; additionally check whether _restore_authored_shape_order re-authors unconditionally, since that determines whether the copy path's tail re-seed ever runs.

**Prior art.** memory project_seed_spine_anchors.md / project_chain_anchor_seed_mixed_rig.md record the seed rules and verdicts path-blind.

### F108 [LOW] Body-reference and physics-XML selection tie-break by filesystem enumeration order (unsorted iterdir/rglob), masked on NTFS the way BUG-04 was masked by the seed
`src/nif_convert.py:2007` · effort S · risk behaviour · lens io-encoding-determinism · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=new, fix=reframe, confidence=high

**Claim.** On a tie the CBBE base body used for the warp, the legacy UBE body fallback (2040-2050) and the per-piece physics XML follow directory enumeration order. NTFS enumerates name-sorted, so output is stable on this machine, but a usvfs-merged listing, exFAT or a network path changes the pick - a whole-run input decided by the filesystem, invisible in any log, exactly the class project_bone_add_order_latent describes ('masked, not fixed').

**Evidence.** 1966: `mod_dirs = [d for d in root.iterdir() if d.is_dir()]` (_iter_femalebody_nifs) -> 2007-2010: `for _, p in sorted(cands, key=lambda t: -t[0]): if _shape_has_3ba_topology(p): ... return p` (stable sort keeps iterdir order among equal hint scores; first accepted wins). 23463: `xmls = list(mesh_dir.rglob("*.xml"))` -> 23531-23539: `for xml in xmls: ... if score > best_score: best_score = score; best_xml = xml` (first equal-score XML wins). Contrast `_sorted_mod_dirs` 24115 `got = sorted(d for d in root.iterdir() if d.is_dir())`, which the project already sorts for the same reason.

**Verifier.** Confirmed: nif_convert.py:1966 `mod_dirs = [d for d in root.iterdir() if d.is_dir()]` unsorted; 2007 `sorted(cands, key=lambda t: -t[0])` is stable so ties keep iterdir order and the first content-valid candidate is returned; 23463 `xmls = list(mesh_dir.rglob("*.xml"))` unsorted and 23537 `if score > best_score` keeps the first equal-score XML; contrast 24115 `_sorted_mod_dirs` already sorts. Memory project_physics_xml_selection_cleared.md and project_bone_add_order_latent.md do not record the enumeration-order class. RISK MISCLASSIFIED: if the probe finds any changed pick, the shipped physics XML/collider bytes or the warp body change - that is 'behaviour' until the probe reads 0, exactly as project_bone_add_order_latent treats sorted(). The finding's own proof admits this.

**Fix.** `sorted()` at the two producers (1966, 23463) so ties resolve by name; a probe on the live modlist decides whether any pick changes.

**Proof it landed.** Probe first (as written); risk downgrades to vert-neutral ONLY if 0 picks change on the live modlist. Otherwise a paired convert per changed piece and a verdict.

**Prior art.** BUG-04 (set-iteration order, fixed with sorted at 12976) and the hash-seed pin are recorded; the directory-enumeration class is not.

### F109 [LOW] `_harden_hdt_xml_for_fsmp` rewrites the author's XML through splitlines()/'\n'.join, so a CRLF file is normalised to LF whenever one block is pruned - contradicting the round-trip helper's byte-for-byte contract
`src/nif_convert.py:24606` · effort S · risk behaviour · lens io-encoding-determinism · verifier: real=confirmed, prior-art=new, fix=sound, confidence=medium

**Claim.** Pruning one shape block from a CRLF-authored physics file rewrites every line ending (and appends a trailing newline) - a second unrequested edit of the author's file, the same spirit as BUG-12's 'do NOT strip the BOM', and it turns every source-vs-ours physics diff into a whole-file diff. Physics-neutral to FSMP, so low.

**Evidence.** 24545: `for line in text.splitlines():` ... 24605-24606: `atomic_write_bytes(xml_path, ("\n".join(out) + "\n").encode(_codec))`; helper contract 24357-24358: `Read an HDT-SMP XML as (text, codec) so it can be written back BYTE-FOR-BYTE where we did not deliberately change it`. The sibling passes preserve bytes (`_make_chains_static` 24410 and `_ensure_cloth_body_collider` 24502 use `text.replace`). tests/test_xml_encoding_roundtrip.py: grep for `\r\n|CRLF`: 0 hits.

**Verifier.** Confirmed: nif_convert.py:24545 `for line in text.splitlines():` and 24605-24606 `atomic_write_bytes(xml_path, ("\n".join(out) + "\n").encode(_codec))` under `if changed:`; helper docstring 24357-24358 promises BYTE-FOR-BYTE where unchanged; siblings 24410 and 24502 use text.replace and preserve endings. tests/test_xml_encoding_roundtrip.py: fixture at 55-58 writes `_XML.encode("utf-8")` (LF); the harden test at 121-124 covers the BOM only, no CRLF fixture (grep for CRLF/\r\n: only the word 'harden' matched). Only fires when a block is actually pruned, and the trailing-newline addition is real. Physics-neutral to FSMP; low is right. Risk 'behaviour' is honest since shipped XML bytes change. Memory: BUG-12 only.

**Fix.** `text.splitlines(keepends=True)` joined with `"".join(out)` and no synthetic trailing newline; add a CRLF fixture with one pruned block to the round-trip test asserting only that block's bytes differ.

**Proof it landed.** Byte test as above (assert on BYTES, per the BUG-12 lesson); reconvert one SMP piece whose author XML is CRLF and diff the shipped XML against the author's - only the pruned block should differ. Mesh untouched (XML-only pass), so no vertex comparison applies.

**Prior art.** Not found; BUG-12's `do NOT` line ('do not fix this by stripping the BOM') is the recorded sibling rule.

### F113 [LOW] The July cross-shape seam SKIN-match still runs by default although it is superseded by #coincident-skin-match and measured inert on copy and marginally negative on body-swap
`src/nif_convert.py:25747` · effort S · risk behaviour · lens pass-contract · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=known-open, fix=sound, confidence=high

**Claim.** A CLEANUP that runs at the wrong point in the chain (before the passes that re-diverge its rows), superseded by the post-write pass with the in-game verdict, kept ON with a measured net-negative and no case for it. Small blast radius, but it is one of the five sink-side repairs in the finding above and the cheapest to retire.

**Evidence.** 25747: `if seam_clusters and not _flag("CBBE2UBE_NO_SEAM_SKIN_MATCH", False):` (default ON; GUI row `CBBE2UBE_NO_SEAM_SKIN_MATCH` exists). memory/project_pass_usefulness_audit_2026_08_17.md §2: copy "fired on 1 of 12 meshes, 21 weight entries ... moved the cross-shape weight-shear count by zero"; body-swap "turning the pass OFF removes 7 shearing verts"; 08-17: "shear verts over 0.5: 5 -> 4 BETTER with the old pass OFF ... there is no measurable case FOR keeping the skin half". 14240 docstring explains why: it runs on pass-1 shape_jobs and "anything placed before [the weight passes] would simply be re-diverged".

**Verifier.** 25747 `if seam_clusters and not _flag("CBBE2UBE_NO_SEAM_SKIN_MATCH", False):` confirmed, GUI row at gui_settings.py:89 (invert=True). The audit memory's 08-23 re-scope (lines 209-225) corrects the magnitude: copy path inert (21 entries, shear 1340->1340), body-swap 'rare but huge where it fires' — 91,984 weight entries on ONE piece, shear 54->46 with the pass OFF, 'magnitude was understated ~4000x'. So 'marginally negative' is wrong: it is large-but-still-negative on body-swap, inert on copy, which strengthens the case to default the skin half OFF. Lines 365-385: 'no measurable case FOR keeping the skin half' as quoted. Risk behaviour honest (weights change on the pieces it fires on); in-game verdict still owed.

**Fix.** Default the skin half OFF (leave the POSITION weld, `NO_SEAM_WELD`, untouched); one layered body-swap piece in game; then delete per the disproven-toggles recipe if the verdict holds.

**Proof it landed.** Weights-only A/B (0 verts moved), serial; cross-shape shear vs author on the reachable population must not worsen (expect -7 on the one piece it fires on); `verify_zero_weight_bones.py`; in-game before commit.

**Prior art.** memory/project_pass_usefulness_audit_2026_08_17.md §2 (08-23 re-scope: inert on copy, large-negative on body-swap)

## 4. Design-level, multi-session  (11 items)

### F006 [HIGH] PRECONDITION B: the `nc.*` namespace IS the test and sibling API (~156 monkeypatch sites over 56 names, 78 reload sites in 22 files, 254 private-name refs, no __all__) -- a star-import shim re-exports none of it, and patches only reach callers whose code still lives in nif_convert.py
`src/nif_convert.py:73` · effort L · risk vert-neutral · lens monolith-seams · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=new, fix=reframe, confidence=medium

**Claim.** Three Python facts decide the split design: (a) `from .m import *` skips every underscore name unless the sub-module defines `__all__`; (b) `monkeypatch.setattr(nc, X, fake)` rebinds nif_convert's dict, and a caller resolves X in ITS OWN module dict (LOAD_GLOBAL), so a patch reaches only callers whose code still lives in nif_convert.py; (c) `importlib.reload(nc)` re-executes nif_convert.py only — a constant that moved to a sub-module is not re-read from the environment. So a naive 'move the function, add a star-import shim' breaks 163 patch sites in a way that is sometimes vacuous (a fake meant to inject data is bypassed, the real function runs), invalidates the 22-file reload idiom, and trips tests/test_incremental_config_floor.py L191 `assert len(helper) >= 250` the moment more than 59 `_flag/_knob` constants leave the file.

**Evidence.** src/nif_convert.py L73 `from .envflags import flag as _flag, knob as _knob` (constants bind at import); grep `__all__` in nif_convert.py: 0 hits. Full counts (not truncated): 163 `setattr((nc|nif_convert|_nc), "...")` sites over 55 distinct names in tests/ (top: `_read_source_hdt_xml_text` 12, `_pynifly` 12, `GROOVE_ONESIDED` 10, `GROOVE_AUTHORED_CAP` 9, `SLEEVE_GARMENT_NOT_EXTREMITY` 8, `_hdt_softbody_shape_names` 7, `atomic_nif_save` 6); 254 distinct `nc._name` and 79 distinct `nc.UPPER` references in tests; 78 `importlib.reload(nc)` sites in 22 test files (e.g. tests/test_chain_welded_torso.py L111 `assert importlib.reload(nc).LEG_CHAIN_GUARD is True`); 27 whole-module `inspect.getsource(nc)` sites; 132 test files import nc. Siblings: src/auto_convert.py L56 `from . import ube_patcher, nif_convert, paths, discovery`; src/hdt_xml_gen.py L757 `from . import nif_convert as _nc  # local: nif_convert imports this module`; overlay_transfer.py L385/L417, build_mod.py L189, cli.py L38, preflight.py L58/L67; 25 scripts/analysis files (`nc._verts_skin_to_world` x8, `nc._shape_global_to_skin` x8, `nc._find_ube_femalebody` x6, `nc._CONFORM_FIT_PROX` x4 ...).

**Verifier.** Substance confirmed; several counts are slightly off. Re-counted over tests/ (254 files) with regex `setattr\(\s*(nc|nif_convert|_nc)\s*,\s*["'](\w+)["']`: 156 patch sites (158 with any quoting) over 56 distinct names in 36 files, not 163/55 -- top names match the finding exactly (_pynifly 12, _read_source_hdt_xml_text 12, GROOVE_ONESIDED 10, GROOVE_AUTHORED_CAP 9, SLEEVE_GARMENT_NOT_EXTREMITY 8, _hdt_softbody_shape_names 7, atomic_nif_save 6). `importlib.reload(nc)`: 78 sites in 22 files (match). Distinct `nc._x`: 254, distinct `nc.UPPER`: 79 (match). `inspect.getsource(nc)`: 26 not 27. Files importing nc: 132 (match). scripts/ files using `nc.`: 31 total, 20 under scripts/analysis (finding says 25). `__all__` absent from nif_convert.py (confirmed). L73 verified verbatim. All seven sibling import lines verified verbatim. tests/test_incremental_config_floor.py L191 `assert len(helper) >= 250` confirmed; 309-250 = 59 constants of headroom is correct. CBBEtoUBE.spec L47-60 documents lz4 and src.gui as lazy-import hiddenimports, as claimed. The three Python facts are correct semantics and the vacuous-fake hazard is concrete in the cited test: tests/test_collider_declared_bones.py L95-101 patches `nc._read_source_hdt_xml_disk` and `nc._read_source_hdt_xml_text` (defs near L25208, physics/XML region) and calls `nc._bust_split_xml_text` (def L15993, bust-split region), whose body calls both names BARE (L16013, L16019) -- if those two regions land in different modules the fakes are bypassed and the test reads the real disk. What I could NOT verify: the per-cluster same-module caller counts (12/9, 6/4, 4/1, ...) depend on the reader's clustering, which I could not reproduce. Prior art: phase1_settled.json already records 'all 309 _flag/_knob constants and 225 literal constants stay in nif_convert.py' and 'import-time binding stays the contract after a split' -- improvement (i) restates settled ground; the star-import/monkeypatch/reload constraints (ii)-(iv) are new. One design hazard the finding misses: with (ii) `from .nif_x import *` in nif_convert and (iii) `from . import nif_convert as nc` at nif_x's top, importing `src.nif_x` FIRST (a test or script) runs nif_x -> nif_convert -> star-import of a partially-initialised nif_x with no `__all__` bound yet -> import-time failure. src/__init__.py is empty, so nothing forces nif_convert first; hdt_xml_gen.py L757 already uses the local-import pattern for exactly this circularity.

**Fix.** Keep (ii)-(v) and add: (vi) sub-modules must NOT bind `nc` at module top -- either import it lazily inside each function (the pattern src/hdt_xml_gen.py L757 already uses) or have src/__init__.py import nif_convert first -- plus one test that imports every `src/nif_*.py` in isolation in a fresh interpreter (`python -c 'import src.nif_x'`), because `from .nif_x import *` in nif_convert against a partially-initialised nif_x fails at import time when nif_x is imported first; (vii) replace the per-cluster caller counts with a generated name->module table (the phase-1 gaps entry asks for the same) so the 'same-module patch site' list is derived, not hand-counted.

**Proof it landed.** As stated (PYTHONHASHSEED=1 golden_output capture/check at tolerance 0 per move commit, pytest --collect-only node-ID diff, pass_map --check, then OLD-vs-NEW exe via exe_parity_convert.py in two scratch out_dirs with no `meshes` dir, verts + per-bone weights per shape, verify_zero_weight_bones.py) -- 'vert-neutral' is the claim to be proved, not a property of the rewrite; the finding itself says step (iii) is not neutral by construction. Add: run the full suite once with each sub-module imported before nif_convert to catch the partial-initialisation failure.

**Prior art.** phase1_settled.json (monolith-seams) already settles improvement (i) -- constants stay in nif_convert.py -- and the import-time binding contract; cite it and drop (i) to a reference. ### F048 [MEDIUM] Extraction order: the in-file call graph has two pure leaves (telemetry, skin-frame) and one hub (physics); leaf-first order minimises test churn, and the per-step proof is a convert, not a diff

(2) One tests helper that concatenates every src module's text for whole-file reads. (3) Execute Plan Step 6 partners for per-function pins. (4) Ratchet test on the count of whole-file nif_convert.py reads.

**Proof it landed.** Tests only: mutation control -- remove a call site in a scratch copy and show both the text and the structural test go red. Any future split is a src change and needs a convert-based vertex compare with PYTHONHASHSEED=1 in one harness (rule 5).

**Prior art.** docs/worklog/PLAN_PASS_CONSOLIDATION.md Step 6 (32 source-text tests, partners owed); memory project_converter_audit_2026_07_26.md:45 (keep both forms)

### F103 [MEDIUM] A single STAGE TABLE can drive both paths, but as three sub-tables over a shared context, not one flat list
`src/nif_convert.py:28734` · effort XL · risk vert-neutral · lens two-path-drift · verifier: real=partly, prior-art=known-open, fix=reframe, confidence=low

**Claim.** Feasible and it fixes the structural cause of findings 1-2, 4-7 and 12: one call site per pass (arguments built once from a per-path context, so kwarg/guard drift is impossible), one failure-reporting contract, the mask becomes the parity guard (no call-graph walk that a dead branch can satisfy - finding 10), and PASS_MAP becomes the table with no depth-1 blind spot. It does NOT remove the 12 phase-2-only passes or any A/B they need; the mask just makes them visible. Cost: ~3,800 lines re-expressed as ~30 stage functions plus a `FitContext` (body verts/normals, source body pair, nipple map, slot bits, name sets) and a per-shape `ShapeState` (source, current, frame, skip mask, flags); the anti-poke/softcloth exclusivity and the chain-guard rollback become guard predicates and a wrapping stage. Three tables, because the units differ: per-shape geometry (state in/out), cross-shape (jobs list), post-write tail (path). Order must be copied from the current labels verbatim - no reordering.

**Evidence.** The stage labels already exist and are the product's order: phase 2 `_stage(label, v)` L28734-28748 emits 20 labels, the copy path `_stage_p1` L6915-6923 emits 10 with 'Same labels phase 2 uses', and PASS_MAP lists both chains. AST probe: zero `_flag`/`_knob`/environ reads inside either function - every tunable is an import-time module constant, and the per-path difference is expressed ONLY by separate constants (copy-only: PHASE1_CONFORM, PHASE1_BUST_CLEARANCE, STATIC_CHAINS, INJECT_UBE_EXTREMITY_REPLACEMENT; phase2-only: 29 incl. PHASE2_CONFORM, CONFORM_MARGIN, REBURY_AUTHORED, LAYER_ORDER_LAST, _MORPHTRI_SCALE, _SRC_NORMAL_FIX). fit_metrics reads env at import too except `_enabled()`/`_band_enabled()` (L96-132), telemetry only. Data-flow that a flat table must carry: `override`/`snapped` threading; `_skip` computed at L28923 and reused by the post-antipoke half L29166; `_bust_driven` (L28968) feeding the antipoke/softcloth if/elif exclusivity (L29055/L29197); `_layer_extra` from a pre-pass (L28403); `_off_p2` frame bookkeeping added L28646 and removed L29512; `_chain.finish()` rollback wrapping seam_weld/coherence/strap/short_edge (L29376-29438); the hf branch's `hf_ef` weighting (L6689-6693).

**Verifier.** The _stage (L28734-28748) and _stage_p1 (L6915-6923) boundaries exist as quoted and are the natural table keys. Not verified: the 'zero _flag/_knob reads inside either function' AST claim and the 29-constant phase2-only list. One material omission: the two stage hooks already differ in contract - phase 2's _stage feeds _tracer and _chain (the rollback checkpoint that wraps seam_weld/coherence/strap/short_edge), _stage_p1 feeds only survival/dump - so a shared runner must first decide whether the copy path GAINS the chain-guard rollback (a behaviour change, not vert-neutral) or the runner carries a per-path checkpoint contract. Prior art: memory project_convert_path_divergence step 3 treats unification as a pack A/B; this finding's separation of table-building (neutral) from mask-flipping (behaviour) is a legitimate reframe but XL and unproven. Keep as a design direction, not as an actionable defect.

**Fix.** Add a step (0): make the two checkpoint hooks one function with an explicit per-path contract (chain-guard on/off), proven vertex-identical, before any stage extraction.

**Proof it landed.** As written, plus: the copy path must NOT acquire _chain rollback or _tracer semantics in step (1) - assert the checkpoint contract per path is unchanged, or declare the change and A/B it.

**Prior art.** memory project_convert_path_divergence step 3; docs/worklog/PLAN_PASS_CONSOLIDATION.md.

AST probe over 78 files / 90 add_argument dests: 0 unread; 65 documented `--options` in TOOL_MAP usage lines all present in code; `x = args.y` assigned-but-never-read: 0; all 31 manual-argv tools consume every option literal they declare. morph_clip_test.py `--sweep` (the memory's dead case) is live at L359 (add_argument) and L406-407 `if a.sweep: return _sweep(...)`. — So nobody re-runs the dead-option hunt on the tracked set; the scratchpad was not probed for this. _(lens scratch probes dead_cli_probe.py + manual-argv single-occurrence probe; project_clip_metric_speedup.md records the --sweep fix)_
- **[harness-hygiene]** A/B-against-the-pack (lens item e): every tracked A/B harness compares two arms from one harness — coherence_repair_ab.py:20-23 and preset_ab_score.py:43-44 say so in their docstrings, morphtri_gate_ab.py adds a same-arm nondeterminism control (L74-82), inflate_census.py isolates `arm_env` (L330-333), zero_weight_pair_ab.py pairs two BULK builds (the permitted pack-vs-pack form), golden_output.py checks against its own capture and re-execs with PYTHONHASHSEED pinned (L449-487), survival_sweep.py sets PYTHONHASHSEED=1 for its children (L141). — The parity rule is honoured in code; the remaining risk is in memory prose (see gaps). _(docstrings and code read this lens; feedback_convert_parity_harness.md)_

- **[harness-hygiene]** tests/test_analysis_repo_root.py pins that every scripts/analysis tool puts the REPO ROOT (not scripts/) on sys.path with a >=15 population floor of its own; the 58-file `sys.path.insert(0, ...)` shim duplication is therefore guarded even though it is a copy. — Not worth a finding; collapsing the shim would also require rewriting that test's pattern. _(tests/test_analysis_repo_root.py L1-60)_
- **[performance]** NIF I/O (opens/saves) and merging the five limb-family wrappers' opens into one open/apply/write — I/O is ~6.5% of a conversion (107 opens 3.2%, 18 saves 3.3%); the five wrappers account for 8 opens, ~0.10 s, 0.25% of the run. A call count is not a cost. _(memory project_converter_speed.md 2026-08-23 profile)_
- **[performance]** _reauthor_nif_fresh (9.5%, 40 of the 58 _copy_shape calls) — merging its rebuilds — Every call site is guarded and fires only when there is work; merging the shape-order rebuild with the conform rebuild would save ~4.7% but shape ORDER is load-bearing for alt-texture binding, so it needs an in-game verdict, not a byte diff. _(memory project_converter_speed.md 2026-08-23; project_alt_texture_index_shift.md)_
- **[performance]** _TorsoCast / standoff band records (record_standoff, record_torso_bands) — the 17.3% telemetry — Already split behind `#standoff-band-audit` (CBBE2UBE_STANDOFF_BAND_AUDIT), DEFAULT OFF, output byte-identical; the gate sits on the branch that BUILDS the cast (fit_metrics L100-133 `_band_enabled`, L1512, L1565). Nothing to re-propose. _(memory project_standoff_band_audit.md; fit_metrics.py L100-133)_
- **[performance]** fit_metrics.clipping / exposed (10.5% + 8.4% in the 08-23 profile) look like telemetry — They are ChainGuard, which measures entry and exit and ROLLS BACK a shape the chain made worse — already the consolidated form (2 measurements per armed shape vs 11 per-pass). PassTracer/StandoffTrace/DisplacementSurvival/GeometryDump are all default OFF (fit_metrics L757, L853, L1043, L1117). _(memory project_converter_speed.md; fit_metrics.py L1262-1291, L1325-1333)_
- **[performance]** add_scale_bone_weights (historical #1 hotspot) — vectorise or cache its per-bone trees — Per-bone KD-trees are already cached per body in `_SCALE_BONE_DATA_CACHE` (L21051-21096); vectorising its loops gave 0 speedup (query-bound). Leave it. _(memory project_converter_speed.md 'Already optimized — do NOT re-target')_
- **[performance]** _weld_cross_shape_seams / _match_seam_skinning quadratic weight rescans — Fixed by indexing weights to {bone: {vert: weight}} — 160.1 s -> 8.78 s, bit-identical digest, on the copy path (78% of files). _(docs/worklog/OPTIMIZATION_LOG.md 'The copy path was never profiled')_
- **[performance]** generate_armor_tri (BODYTRI morphs) per-slider IDW weight rebuild — Hoisted + einsum + tolist: 11.80 s -> 5.02 s, SHA-256-identical morph offsets. _(docs/worklog/OPTIMIZATION_LOG.md 07-30)_
- **[performance]** RAY_CHUNK size (256 -> unchunked) as a speed lever — ~2% spread in speed, 33x memory margin; the default 512 was already right. Measured non-result. _(docs/worklog/OPTIMIZATION_LOG.md 'Chunk size: a measured non-result')_
- **[performance]** The ray cast itself (_pairs via sparse_distance_matrix, radius tiers, Lagrange ray-line cull, region restriction) — Three exact optimisations already shipped (4.0x, 1.6x, 2.0x; 391 ms -> ~120 ms per region measurement); remaining cast cost is structural (ball query of radius tmax around every ray origin). What is left is not doing the work twice, and the torso union cast already does that for the (now OFF) band records. _(CHANGELOG 1.3-alpha 'Performance — the ray cast'; OPTIMIZATION_LOG 'Why the remaining cast cost is structural'; fit_metrics L254-351)_
- **[performance]** Skeleton bone-set normalisation (1.9M _norm_bone calls, ~5-6%) — Cached as a (raw set, normalised frozenset) pair keyed on identity; shipped 1.2.5. _(memory project_converter_speed.md; CHANGELOG L1294-1297)_
- **[performance]** Source HDT XML re-read per shape (24.7% hotspot) and the morph-TRI name gate re-parse (7 passes, 14.0 s -> 0.95 s) — Both memoised (`_HDT_XML_TEXT_CACHE` L25127, keyed on (path, mtime_ns, size) and cleared per convert_nif; `_MORPH_TRI_NAME_CACHE` L9498 keyed on path+mtime+size because the converter writes TRIs mid-run). _(memory project_converter_speed.md 'HDT-XML memo'; CHANGELOG L598-608)_
- **[performance]** _stacked_layer_groups / _stacked_layer_plan (which rebuild `_KD(bV)` per ranked layer at L21934) — Measured 0.27% even on a five-shape piece, its worst case. Not worth targeting. _(memory project_converter_speed.md 2026-08-11)_
- **[performance]** Worker pool size (23 vs 16) and the RAM-capped default_worker_count — A careful 20-mod / 775-NIF interleaved A/B was a TIE (inside noise); settling it needs two FULL ~2.2 h runs. The RAM cap shipped as oversubscription insurance, not as a speedup. Do not re-run at small scale. _(memory project_converter_speed.md 'WORKER-POOL SIZE 23 vs 16')_
- **[performance]** Multiprocessing granularity and pickling (lens item e, apart from the per-mod barrier) — Tasks are per NIF and pickle only (src Path, dst Path, body-ref path str, slot int, alt-texture names) — auto_convert L1729-1734; the body ref NIF, body OSD and CBBE->UBE delta are loaded once per WORKER through module caches (`_UBE_BODY_REF_CACHE`, `_OSD_CACHE`, `_CBBE_UBE_DELTA_CACHE`) and pre-warmed one task per worker (L96-134, L137-182); ConvertResult carries paths, strings and small dicts (L6282-6295), no arrays. submit()-per-item has no chunksize and none is needed at ~20 s mean per task. The shared pool persists across mods (L2900). Nothing to change here. _(auto_convert.py L60-182, L185-305, L1729-1734, L2887-2915; OPTIMIZATION_LOG 'The pool is NOT rebuilt per mod')_
- **[performance]** _rim_edges (fit_metrics L395-402) python dict loop over every garment triangle — Sized in this audit: 220 ms per 100k triangles in python vs 146 ms for the obvious vectorisation (np.unique axis=0) — 1.5x on a once-per-shape call is not worth a change; the dense `_rim_distance` it feeds is the real cost (finding above). _(scratch micro-bench, verbatim copy of the function, set-equality checked)_
- **[performance]** VFS mesh index rebuilt per batch (memory candidate 2, ~16% of the serial upfront cost) — The index built during source selection is now reused for the convert (`_BATCH_MESH_INDEX`, auto_convert L2939-2944 'no second modlist walk'); a cross-run disk cache remains unbuilt because folder mtimes miss nested mesh edits (wrong source NIF = sacrificed output). Outside this lens's two modules; not re-proposed. _(auto_convert.py L2929-2966; memory project_converter_speed.md candidate 2)_

## What this audit did not cover

- **monolith-seams:** The 78 functions (3,147 lines) my name/section clustering left 'unassigned' were placed by their banner section and call neighbourhood in the module findings (warp sub-steps -> fit-geometry, conform-weights internals -> weights, bake helpers -> skin-frame, sanitisers -> writer, predicates -> core) but not individually verified; a follow-up should assign each by reading its callers and emit a machine-readable name->module table the widened guards (Precondition A) can pin. I did not measure the runtime cost of `nc.`-attribute reads in hot loops (expected negligible; the 4-influence cap and the K=16 morph loops are the places to profile — project_converter_speed says NIF I/O is only 6.5%). I ran no conversion and no test: every 'loud vs silent' claim about a pin is from reading the assertion, not from breaking it — the mutation-in-a-worktree probe in Precondition A is how a follow-up should confirm each one before relying on it. scripts/analysis (25 importers) and research/ were counted, not read, for private-name use beyond the grep list; docs/PIPELINE.md's 46 backticked helper names were not checked for section/line references that a move would stale. The `_stage*` closures capture locals of the entry functions (`_surv_p1`, `_dump_p1`), which is why they stay — but STAGE_DUMP/survival tracing across a moved pass was not traced end to end. Finally, the rule-5 proof recipe depends on golden_output.py's pinned-seed re-exec (it pins PYTHONHASHSEED=0 internally) agreeing with a PYTHONHASHSEED=1 shell — both arms of every comparison must be produced the same way, in one harness, or the ~53-weight-row residual nondeterminism will masquerade as a failed move.
- **two-path-drift:** Static lens only: no conversion was run, so every geometry divergence (frame reconciliation, anchor placement, scale-bone gate, HDT only_loose) is a population question whose size is unmeasured - each proof field names the read-only census to run first. Two direct-call helpers were not opened (`_copy_shape` beyond its transform/repair lines; `_finalize_hdt_physics` internals), so any drift INSIDE shared helpers keyed on path-dependent state (e.g. `skip_geometry_repair=LAYER_ORDER_LAST` reaching `_copy_shape` only from phase 2, L30001) is out of scope here and belongs to the write-time lens. Reachability of the per-path flag pairs (PHASE1_CONFORM has no GUI row while NO_PHASE2_CONFORM and PHASE1_BUST_CLEARANCE do; CONFORM_MARGIN, RESKIN_KEEP, MORPHTRI_SCALE, REBURY_AUTHORED, CUIRASS_INFLATE, TRI_BOTH_WEIGHTS have none) was noted but not scored - the reachability lens should own it, with the observation that half of several pairs is user-reachable and the other half is not. A follow-up should (1) extend tests/test_convert_path_parity.py's `_reachable` to also stop at `_reauthor_nif_fresh` and assert per-BRANCH reach (rebuild vs verbatim), since finding 10 shows a pass can satisfy the guard through a branch production never takes; (2) run the frame census and the spine-anchor global walk split by path before any of the geometry findings is acted on; (3) if the stage table is attempted, capture the per-mod noise floor on the UNCHANGED tree first, because the vert-neutral proof depends on it.
- **pass-contract:** No conversion was run, so every damage/survival number here is the project's own from 2026-08-23 stage dumps; the damage ledger memory pools both paths (63 shapes) and does not publish a per-path penetration table, so finding 1's '~1938 copy-path penetrations left unrepaired' is an inference from (total 6659 - body-swap 4721) and should be re-measured per path with `damage_ledger.py` grouped by last stage before being quoted. The fine-animation sub-branch (warp_hf/inflate_hf, ~20% of copy-path shapes) has no cleanup and no rigidity at all — its 'would fire on 51/51 shapes' probe is recorded but untested in game; this lens did not grade it beyond noting the parity guard cannot see sub-branch asymmetry. Pass BODIES beyond docstrings and call sites were not re-read, so any repair-in-disguise inside a producer (e.g. `clear_armor_outside_body`'s 263 lines, `_copy_shape`'s 433) is out of scope; a follow-up should AST-walk each producer for calls to `_smooth_push_field`/`_smooth_vertex_field`/`_recompute_vertex_normals` and classify those inner steps. The post-write weight tail was graded on docstrings and the usefulness audit only — a save-by-save write ledger (`leather_trace.py` style) over the 28-piece sample would give the producer/cleanup split for weights the same footing the geometry ledger has. `_conform_fitted_to_body`'s SELFINT_REPAIR half (cleanup of warp self-intersection on single-shape stacks, producer never attributed by measurement) was classified but not investigated. Finding 10's TRI-write reorder must first check the BODYTRI extra-data ordering constraint in PIPELINE §2d; this lens did not trace `_pick_bodytri_carriers` and the extra-data writers. Rule 5 applies to every improvement above: none is behaviour-neutral until a convert-based vertex comparison says so (byte compare invalid; PYTHONHASHSEED=1 for source runs; A/B within one harness, never against the pack).
- **tunable-inventory:** Three things this lens could not settle statically. (1) The (E) sub-class 'read but multiplied by zero or clamped away downstream' (the QUERY_K shape) is invisible to a gate walk; proving a knob live needs a per-knob perturbation convert - set each row-less knob to 2x default on a piece whose band it covers and assert a non-zero vertex diff against control in one exe_parity_convert.py harness (both weights, PYTHONHASHSEED=1 for source runs). A knob_liveness.py over the 10 GUI knobs first is the cheapest high-value follow-up. (2) The (D) label-vs-pass check covered only the suspect families (conform pair, seams, jiggle, chain guard, anchors, layer ride); a full audit of all 72 bool rows against the PASS_MAP stage each constant guards - and of the rows whose constant is used only inside convert_nif_phase2 (CLEARANCE_DIFFERENTIAL L29080, JIGGLE_CLEARANCE_ENABLED L29091, LAYERED_ANTIPOKE_ENABLED L28400, SMP_COLLISION_ONLY_ANTIPOKE L29012, MIXED_CLOTH_CLEARANCE L29037) whose tooltips do not say 'body-swap path only' - belongs with the path-divergence lens. (3) The class-B split into 'safety invariant' vs 'pass under question' needs the owner; all 53 members are listed but only min_push is argued. Also unverified: whether the SOFTCLOTH_OWN_PLANE branch's enclosing handlers are silent (the _inflate_cloth_over_bust_butt call sites were not read) - the exception lens should check before anyone trusts a byte-identical arm from it.
- **settings-plumbing:** This lens did not resolve the 301 env-only (no Setting row) variables to the passes they gate or classify which are diagnostics vs. real opt-ins -- that is the reachability lens; the per-read list (module, line, scope, idiom, registry membership) is in the scratch env_reads.json for it. It did not audit the overlay-transfer worker pool's env plumbing or fit_metrics' import-time telemetry flags beyond noting they are raw module-level reads. No conversion was run, so every 'proof' is a recipe, not a result; the spawn probe only imported nif_convert in a worker. A follow-up should (1) decide headless precedence (json vs env) before any code moves, since finding 1's fix and finding 2's run_config both depend on it; (2) build the Setting.const map, which unlocks a general code/GUI default-agreement ratchet and the effective-config dump in one step; (3) re-run warn_prints.py after routing the 8 sites and assert 0 PRINT-ONLY WARN-class prints remain, then extend the census to non-WARN worker messages (e.g. the 'dropped shape' and 'skipped' notices) which this pass did not classify.
- **swallowed-failures:** (1) Print-only voices were not censused as a class: a handler that `print`s to a pool worker's stderr and does not call the recorder is invisible in the frozen exe (memory: worker prints vanish), yet reads as voiced to every classifier here and in the existing guard; finding 9 is one instance found by reading, a follow-up should AST-count handlers whose only voice is `print` and whose try body touches geometry/partitions/save. (2) Silent handlers in gui.py (58), auto_convert.py (59), ube_patcher.py (24), overlay_transfer.py (19) were triaged by span and call names only; the alt-texture ones are reported, the rest looked like discovery/IO best-effort but were not read one by one. (3) Nothing here proves any handler currently FIRES — the task forbade a convert; the raise-injection test in finding 12 is how to turn each structural finding into a live one, and the first run of that test against the current tree is the measurement. (4) `_note_pass_failure` de-duplicates identical entries per piece and prints only the first 3 per kind per process; if a follow-up needs per-shape counts inside one piece, labels must carry the shape name (as some already do). (5) Reachability of each site was established by reading its callers rather than by running `tests/test_convert_path_parity.py`'s `_reachable(..., stop=...)`; every reported site is on a helper called from both entry functions, but a follow-up voicing the phase-2-only blocks should confirm with the walker.
- **io-encoding-determinism:** No conversion was run (read-only audit), so finding 1 is a traced mechanism with a proof recipe, not a reproduction; the follow-up is the in-process mechanism test (hold the shared XML in two states, convert the same `_1` twice, compare w_content) BEFORE any pool-scale rerun, then bisect by clearing the dst-read fallback. The plugin-string codec class (finding 4) needs a read-only census of MOD2-5/MAST subrecords with bytes >= 0x80 across the live load order to size it (it may be 0 on this modlist, in which case it stays a latent correctness fix with a unit test). Numpy/scipy tie-breaking and BLAS accumulation were not probed; if the XML race does not account for all 53 rows, the next candidate is the 4-influence cap's exact-tie handling under different predecessor state, not float order (the residual is bimodal). tests/ hits of the I/O probe were not individually resolved. scripts/install_rcs.py's text-mode rewrites of MO2's modlist/plugins were not checked against MO2's own line-ending expectation. The `_reachable` walker was not needed here and no dead-code claim is made in this lens.
- **test-quality:** (1) No executed-line coverage was produced: pytest-cov is not installed and installing it was out of scope for a read-only pass. A follow-up should run `pip install pytest-cov` in a venv and `python -m pytest -q --cov=src --cov-report=term-missing -p no:cacheprovider` once (~3 min) to replace the name-based 77/308 estimate with real line coverage of nif_convert.py, and to see whether the 27 zero-named passes are executed indirectly through helpers (they are not executed through the entry points, since no test calls those). (2) The 144 per-function inspect.getsource pins were counted, not individually classified as load-bearing vs wording-only; the 07-26 audit says some are load-bearing, so Step 6 of PLAN_PASS_CONSOLIDATION needs a per-site read before any are removed. (3) The 29 unpinned default-ON constants were identified by regex over test text; a handful may be exercised via monkeypatch without a default assertion, which my regex would count as unpinned -- the structural agreement test in finding 6 makes this moot. (4) I did not run mutation checks on the 27 zero-test passes to prove they are reachable at runtime (PASS_MAP says they are called; rule 6 says a pass absent from the map can still run via a helper, and conversely a mapped pass can be gated off -- e.g. `_stack_probe` is behind PANEL_RIGID_RIDE). (5) tests/ for auto_convert.py (5604 lines) and ube_patcher.py (4653 lines) were only touched where the sweeps hit them; a per-module coverage-gap pass like finding 2 for those modules was not done. (6) The 11 GUI rows read via raw os.environ (BUST_CLEAR, SEAM_WELD_TOL, GLOW_RIDE_MAX, ...) need a reachability check by the flag/knob lens; from the test lens they are simply outside every default-agreement test.
- **harness-hygiene:** (1) Nothing was executed: each 0/0 claim is a read code path; the proof recipes (run against an empty out-mod dir; move golden/pieces.json aside) are the confirmation and take minutes. (2) The 191 scratchpad probes were sampled, not censused, for dead options, floors and A/B-vs-pack; only the environ.clear, .ps1/.sh and the four cited gate/harness files were opened, and the 211 .py files under the session temp dirs were located by name only. (3) A memory-level contradiction outside the repo: project_scratchpad_tooling.md:182 and feedback_single_batch_parity.md:69 instruct 'prove the control arm reproduces what is deployed with vert_delta.py', while feedback_convert_parity_harness.md records a fixed 0.265u harness-vs-shipped residual and says never to diff a test convert against the pack — a follow-up should reconcile the two before anyone runs vert_delta.py as a control. (4) Unverified and out of lens scope but worth one run: scripts/build_exe.ps1:31 sets `$ErrorActionPreference = \"Stop\"` and L45-46 does `& python -c \"import PyInstaller\" 2>$null; if (-not $?)` — under Windows PowerShell 5.1 a redirected native stderr becomes an ErrorRecord and can terminate before the `if`, which would make the documented 'installs PyInstaller if missing' branch unreachable on exactly the machine that needs it; test on a venv without PyInstaller. (5) The shingle probe used 6-token shingles at Jaccard>=0.5 over top-level defs >=8 lines; inline main() bodies (where the 23 out-mod-root derivations live) were counted by regex, so G4/G5 member lists are idiom hits, not proven token-identical blocks. (6) The docstring probe's 'reads/pass/exit' detection is keyword-based (like the generator it criticises); the 29/18/11/20 counts are upper bounds pending the contract block.
- **performance:** No dynamic measurement was possible (read-only audit, no conversion permitted), so every share-of-wall-clock number here is the 2026-08-23 cProfile recorded in memory (one 5-shape body-swap cuirass, 40.8 s, band audit OFF) or a scratch micro-bench at stated sizes; a follow-up must run ONE serial cProfile per path (`--workers 1`, temp output, quiet machine) on the same two pieces and sort by cumtime AND tottime before acting on findings 3-5. Specifically unanswered: (1) the split inside the 12.1% full-vector limb match between the per-shape body-index rebuild, the cast, and the python dict work — finding 3 gives the function names to read; (2) `_copy_shape` at 14.6% / 58 calls was not decomposed statically — its grep shows one per-shape python rebuild of the source weight list (L23358-23360) and `_install_skin`'s per-bone `setShapeWeights` listcomp (L2417), but no body-dependent recomputation; (3) the weight tail as a CLASS — 38 sites rebuild a per-vertex dict-of-bones from `bone_weights` and 19 sites write back through `setShapeWeights` listcomps, each pass re-opening and re-saving the NIF; I/O is settled at 6.5% but the python marshalling share has never been measured (look for `<listcomp>`, `dict.get`, `int`, `float` tottime under the `_match_*`/`_sync_*`/`_conform_fitted_to_body` frames); (4) the non-NIF third of a ~2 h run (discovery, ESP generation, merge, postflight) has never been timed (OPTIMIZATION_LOG plan item 4) and is outside this lens's modules; (5) `--incremental`'s floor still uses max mtime over ALL src/*.py (auto_convert L322) even though NIF bytes depend only on a subset of modules — memory candidate 3 / Tier 1A, a dev-loop cost rather than a run cost, not evaluated here. Also not verified: whether the ChainGuard `exposed()` measurement (2 per armed shape) and `minimum_push`'s own iterations reuse anything — they cannot, the geometry differs between them, so they were not raised.
- **docs-currency:** Not covered: docs/DESIGN_JIGGLE.md and the nine docs/worklog files beyond the env-var scan (they self-describe as dated snapshots, and docs/README.md:26-29 says DESIGN/METRICS win on conflict); the CHANGELOG's released sections (a follow-up could check whether 1.3's 'off by default' claims are annotated where a later promotion flipped them, e.g. the four 08-22 promotions and `ride_body_floor`); GUI tooltip/hint text in gui_settings.py (several carry measurement numbers and 'OFF by default and NOT yet judged' hints that will rot on the next promotion — the same header class as finding 5, worth folding into the proposed test); the `sNN_` stage-index numbers quoted in nif_convert.py comments (PIPELINE.md:285-287 says the index differs per path, so comments citing `s04_conform`/`s07_antipoke` are path-specific — not resolved here); comment currency inside auto_convert.py, ube_patcher.py and gui.py beyond the identifier scan; and the live deployed settings file, which is outside the repo (PIPELINE §6's 'no overrides' claim was checked only against the code default, not the deployed JSON). Suggested follow-ups, in order of value: (1) a test that parses the backticked pass names in PIPELINE §2/§3 and asserts existence + PASS_MAP order, with a negative control; (2) extend tests/test_promoted_defaults.py with the OPT-IN-header scan and a label-exists check for every setting name quoted in CHANGELOG Unreleased; (3) add IN_GAME_BUGS.md, scratchpad_handoff_*/ and scripts/analysis/underbust_census*.json to repo_hygiene.NEVER_TRACKED; (4) rewrite the two entry-point docstrings from PASS_MAP, then rebuild the exe (docstrings ship in the PYZ).
- **packaging-deploy:** No PyInstaller build was executed (it would write under the repo), so exe-level reproducibility after setting PYTHONHASHSEED/SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH is proposed with its proof recipe, not measured; only base_library.zip could be compared across existing commits. docs/PASS_MAP.md churns ~350 renumbered lines on every nif_convert commit (7 of the last 30) because it embeds line numbers — a low-value maintainability item I could not anchor to a stable current line, so it is left for the docs lens. Not examined: THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md against the actually bundled set (charset_normalizer, Tcl/Tk, OpenBLAS); GitHub branch-protection state (the worklog says none); the other legacy scripts (install_rcs.py, run_patcher.*, AddUBERaces.pas) beyond confirming nothing references them; the .pynifly vendoring/upgrade procedure. A follow-up should run two clean builds with the env set and diff exe/base_library.zip with the CArchive reader, then wire the deploy hash check and the build stamp together so the bug log's attribution header is generated rather than typed.
